# FM-Agent Qwen 自验证 mismatch 审计与 Bug 清单

> 生成口径：以 baseline `7d490cbe14486dfa741a22086bf1383fbc280ec7` 的最终 Qwen 产物为输入，对 127 条 MISMATCH 的 SPEC、推导 POST、validator 报告和 probe 做静态分类。
> “confirmed”只表示 probe 观察到实现与生成 SPEC 不同，不等于确认了产品 bug。

## 总括

本次验证覆盖 **457** 个函数，得到 **330 MATCH / 127 MISMATCH / 0 ERROR**，MISMATCH 率为 **27.79%**。127 条 MISMATCH 均有 validator 结果：**113 confirmed / 14 not_confirmed / 0 error**。

按参考清单的四层审计口径重新复核后，分类如下：

| 审计类别 | 数量 | 占 MISMATCH | 含义 |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| 推理误判 | 18 | 14.17% | 代码/可达性理解错误，或 validator 已 not_confirmed |
| SPEC 错误 | 48 | 37.80% | 差异可能可复现，但生成 SPEC 无依据地强化了契约 |
| 契约待确认 | 13 | 10.24% | 差异真实，仓库证据不足以选择期望行为 |
| 实现缺陷候选 | 48 | 37.80% | 有独立格式、调用链、数据完整性或安全影响，值得补测试 |

高置信度误报下界（推理误判 + SPEC 错误）为 **66/127 = 51.97%**。若把契约待确认也视为不可直接修复，则当前进入修复候选队列的是 **48/127 = 37.80%**。该分类是静态审计，不是假定存在外部 ground truth。

## 主要成因

1. **生成 SPEC 把容错偏好写成硬契约。** 很多条目要求任意畸形 JSON、非 UTF-8、BaseException、非 dict Mapping 或超出 schema 的值都必须 never-raise，缺少调用方或格式标准证据。
2. **推导 POST 与可达性分析仍会误读。** 14 条 validator not_confirmed 直接证明了控制流、命名规则、SQLite 语义、重试逻辑或 caller 保证被忽略。
3. **自指 oracle 不能证明产品 bug。** validator 大多使用生成 SPEC 作为 expected；confirmed 只证明实现与该 SPEC 不同。
4. **边界格式和错误处理是本轮高频主题。** 非对象 JSON、无效 UTF-8、路径标准化、临时文件原子性、TOML/JSON schema 和 trace 状态记录反复出现。
5. **抽取/调用图问题具有级联效应。** 函数名碰撞、注释解析、跨行注释、Erlang edge key 和补充边丢失会污染后续 layer、SPEC 和验证。

## 建议的验证器修正顺序

1. SPEC 的每条 hard post-condition 必须携带 caller、README、schema、标准或测试证据；其余只能标记为 hypothesis。
2. 在 implication 判断前先验证 counterexample 是否满足 pre-condition 和真实输入 schema。
3. validator 使用 `SPEC_INVALID / INFERENCE_ERROR / CONTRACT_AMBIGUOUS / IMPLEMENTATION_BUG` 四态，替代 confirmed/not_confirmed 二态。
4. 将 14 个 not_confirmed 作为回归评测集，优先修复控制流和可达性误读。
5. 对实现缺陷候选先转成维护者认可的独立测试，再修改源码。

## 分类说明

- `推理误判`：actual POST、控制流或可达性读取错误，或 validator 已明确 not_confirmed。
- `SPEC 错误`：probe 可复现实现与 SPEC 的差异，但 SPEC 缺少独立契约依据。
- `契约待确认`：差异存在，但应由维护者决定行为并补测试。
- `实现缺陷候选`：有独立协议、格式、真实调用影响或数据/安全边界支持；仍不是最终定罪。

## 逐条 MISMATCH 清单

每项给出 Qwen 清单编号、原始 bug id、SPEC、推导 POST/result、validator 报告与 probe。链接保留完整原文，正文仅放摘录。

### `dashboard-py`

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-001 — `dashboard-py--State::_ingest_event`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/State::_ingest_event.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/State::_ingest_event.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--State::_ingest_event.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--State::_ingest_event.py)
- 触发/冲突：Ingesting an OpenAI-shape llm_call event whose usage carries a non-null prompt_cache_hit_tokens alongside an explicitly null prompt_cache_miss_tokens makes State._ingest_event raise TypeError on totals['input'] += None, because dict.get returns the present null value instead of the prompt_tokens - hit default, violating the spec's guarantee that null fields never cause a raise.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：ev is a JSON object decoded from one complete non-blank line of the structured trace event log; it may omit any field or carry null fields, and may arrive while the monitored pipeline is still appending to the log. The State instance is constructed with its per-stage/per-status event counts, first/last event timestamp bounds, token totals, native cost, verification verdict counters, recent-event history, and LLM-status history initialized and ready to be updated.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Updates the state's aggregates to reflect exactly this single event so that the caller's incremental aggregation over the whole log stays consistent. The per-stage and per-status event count for the event's stage and status advances by exactly one; an event whose stage or status field is absent is tallied under a dedicated placeholder category rather than dropped. The first/last event timestamp bounds widen monotonically to include every event ingested so far and never shrink. For events carrying LLM usage data, the token totals advance by the input, output, cache-read, and cache-write amounts the event reports regardless of which token-accounting schema variant it uses; when a reported input figure conflates cached and new input, it is split into disjoint amounts that sum to the reported total so no token is counted twice; the native cost advances by the USD cost of the reported usage under the event's model; and a prompt-cache sample (cache-read amount, total input amount) is appended to the shared sample window only when the event reports a positive total input, with the sample's cache component never exceeding its total component. The state records the model identifier of the first LLM event that carries one, and later events never overwrite it. For events carrying a verification outcome, exactly one verification verdict counter advances by one according to the outcome — t…
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：After execution of _ingest_event(self, ev), the following invariants hold across all execution paths: 1. TIMESTAMP BOUNDS (always executed): Let start = _parse_iso(ev.get('start_time')) and end = _parse_iso(ev.get('end_time')). - self.first_event_time == min(old_first_event_time, start) if start is not None, else old_first_event_time. Formally: (end is not None (old_last is None end > old_last)) self.last_event_time == end; otherwise self.last_event_time == old_last. 2. STAGE/STATUS COUNTER (always executed): Let stage = ev.get('stage', '?') and status = ev.get('status', '?'). self.stage_counts[stage][status] == old_stage_counts[stage][status] + 1. All other stage_counts entries are unchanged. 3. IF ev.get('type') == 'llm_call' (LLM branch): a. model_seen: Let md = ev.get('metadata', {}) and model = md.get('model'). If model is truthy and old self.model_seen was falsy, then self.model_seen == model; otherwise self.model_seen is unchanged. b. TOKEN TOTALS: Let usage = md.get('usage') or {}. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-002 — `dashboard-py--State::_ingest_opencode`

- 结论：**契约待确认**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/State::_ingest_opencode.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/State::_ingest_opencode.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--State::_ingest_opencode.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--State::_ingest_opencode.py)
- 触发/冲突：When an error record (or a response record with a non-200 status) carries its own _purpose and a correlated request with a purpose exists for the same (trace_file, call_id) key, State._ingest_opencode emits the correlated request's purpose as the LLM-status label because req.get("purpose") is evaluated before rec.get("_purpose"), contrary to the spec which requires the record's own fields to take priority with request details only as a fallback.
- 成因复核：实现与生成 SPEC 确有差异，但仓库现有证据不足以决定产品应采用哪种行为。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：rec is a JSON object decoded from one complete non-blank line of a raw OpenCode trace log file; trace_file, when given, identifies the name of that source file. The record may be a request, a response, or an error entry, may omit any field, and may use variant field-naming conventions across plugin versions. The State instance is constructed with its OpenCode-attributed aggregates — pending-request registry, OpenCode token totals, OpenCode cost, OpenCode call count, LLM-status history, and the shared prompt-cache sample window — initialized and ready to be updated.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Updates the state's OpenCode-attributed aggregates to reflect exactly this single record, governed by the record's kind, so that the caller's incremental aggregation over all trace files stays consistent. Variant field-naming conventions are normalized so a record whose fields carry a plugin-version-specific prefix behaves identically to the equivalent plain-named record. A request record carrying a call identifier becomes eligible for correlation: its timestamp, model, endpoint, and purpose are remembered under the record's own source-file identification — or the caller-supplied filename when the record carries none — paired with its call identifier, so later records sharing the same file-and-identifier pair inherit those details. Request records contribute nothing to the LLM-status history or to token, cost, or call-count totals, and a request record lacking a call identifier contributes nothing at all. An error record appends exactly one entry to the LLM-status history recording an error outcome with the record's own fields, falling back to correlated request details where its own fields are absent. A response record carrying a non-success HTTP status code likewise appends exactly one entry recording an error outcome. A response record carrying LLM usage data appends exactly one entry recording a success outcome, advances the OpenCode call count by exactly one, advances the…
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Let rec_norm = _strip_star(rec) denote the star-prefix-stripped copy of the input record (the caller's original mapping is never mutated). Let kind = rec_norm.get('_kind'), call_id = rec_norm.get('_id'), and trace_key = rec_norm.get('_trace_file') or trace_file. Exactly one of the following mutually exclusive paths is taken: 1. REQUEST PATH (kind == 'request' and call_id is not None): The pending-request registry self._opencode_requests is updated so that self._opencode_requests[(trace_key, call_id)] equals {'ts': _parse_iso(rec_norm.get('_ts')), 'model': rec_norm.get('model'), 'url': rec_norm.get('_url'), 'purpose': rec_norm.get('_purpose')}. If an entry already existed at that key it is overwritten. No other State attribute (token totals, cost, call count, LLM-status history, prompt-cache window) is modified. The function returns None. 2. ERROR PATH (kind == 'error'): Let req = self._opencode_requests.get((trace_key, call_id), {}). Exactly one entry is appended to the LLM-status history via self._push_llm_status with source='opencode', status='error', ts=_parse_iso(rec_norm.get('_ts')) or req.get('ts'), label=req.get('purpose') or rec_norm.get('_purpose') or rec_norm.get('_url') or 'opencode', model=rec_norm.get('model') or req.get('model'), code=_trace_value(rec_norm,'_status','status','status_code') or 'ERR', detail=_trace_value(rec_norm,'_error','error','message'). The pe…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-003 — `dashboard-py--State::scan_bugs`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/State::scan_bugs.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/State::scan_bugs.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--State::scan_bugs.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--State::scan_bugs.py)
- 触发/冲突：A bug-validation .result.json containing valid JSON that is not a dict (e.g. [1, 2, 3]) makes State.scan_bugs() crash with an unhandled AttributeError at d.get('confirmation_status') instead of skipping the file and completing with all three counters set.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：The State instance has been constructed. It may already hold ingested pipeline event aggregates (per-stage status counts), and the bug-validation directory may or may not exist or contain result files. The method may be called repeatedly across dashboard refresh cycles.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Recomputes the three bug-validation outcome counters so that after return they exactly reflect the current contents of the bug-validation directory combined with the ingested event aggregates: bugs_confirmed equals the number of readable, well-formed result files in the bug-validation directory whose confirmation status affirms confirmation; bugs_not_confirmed equals the number of readable, well-formed result files whose confirmation status is negated, absent, or unrecognized; bugs_pending equals max(0, total recorded bug-validation stage events − (bugs_confirmed + bugs_not_confirmed)), i.e. the number of completed bug-validation calls that have no classified result file yet. Result files that cannot be read or parsed contribute to neither the confirmed nor the not-confirmed count. When the bug-validation directory is missing or empty and no bug-validation stage events have been recorded, all three counters are 0. Repeated calls with no intervening change to the directory or event aggregates yield identical counter values. The method reads the bug-validation directory and its result files only; it never creates, modifies, or deletes any bug-validation output.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Upon normal completion (self.bug_dir.exists() is True and no unhandled exception occurs): 1. self.bugs_confirmed equals the count of files matching '*.result.json' in self.bug_dir that were successfully opened and parsed as a JSON dict, and whose lowercased 'confirmation_status' value (defaulting to '' if absent or None) does NOT contain the substring 'not' but DOES contain the substring 'confirm'. 2. self.bugs_not_confirmed equals the count of successfully parsed JSON-dict result files whose lowercased confirmation_status either (a) contains the substring 'not', or (b) contains neither 'not' nor 'confirm' (the else branch). Files that failed to open or parse (any Exception during open/json.load) are excluded from both counters. 3. self.bugs_pending equals max(0, S - (self.bugs_confirmed + self.bugs_not_confirmed)), where S is the sum of all values in self.stage_counts.get('bug_validation', {}). If the 'bug_validation' key is absent, S = 0 and bugs_pending = 0. 4. All three attributes (bugs_confirmed, bugs_not_confirmed, bugs_pending) are set as integers 0. Early-return path (self.bug_dir.exists() is False): - self.bugs_confirmed is set to 0. - self.bugs_not_confirmed is set to 0. - self.bugs_pending is NOT assigned; it retains whatever value it held prior to the call (or remains undefined if never previously set). - The method returns None. Exception path: - If any successful…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-004 — `dashboard-py--State::tail_events`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/State::tail_events.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/State::tail_events.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--State::tail_events.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--State::tail_events.py)
- 触发/冲突：An event-log line containing bytes that are not valid UTF-8 (e.g. 0xff) makes the strict-UTF-8 text-mode line iterator in State.tail_events() raise an uncaught UnicodeDecodeError, violating the spec's guarantees that malformed lines never raise and never interrupt ingestion of subsequent lines.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：The State instance is constructed and may be called repeatedly across dashboard refresh cycles; the state maintains its own byte-offset cursor into the structured trace event log across calls. The event log, if present, is a UTF-8 text file holding one JSON-encoded pipeline event per line, and the monitored pipeline may append to it concurrently while this function runs.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns after ingesting every event record appended to the structured trace event log since the previous invocation, updating the state's per-stage event counts, in-flight stage counts, token totals, native cost, verification verdict counters, recent-event history, and LLM-status history accordingly. Across successive invocations no event record in the log is ingested more than once; if the log's length has shrunk below the position ingested so far, ingestion resumes from the beginning of the file so the aggregates reflect the current log content. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-005 — `dashboard-py--State::tail_opencode`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。

- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：The State instance is constructed and may be called repeatedly across dashboard refresh cycles; the state maintains, per raw OpenCode log file, its own byte-offset cursor across calls. Each log file under the OpenCode trace directory, if present, is a UTF-8 text file holding one JSON-encoded raw LLM request/response record per line, and the monitored pipeline may create new files or append to existing ones concurrently while this function runs.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns after ingesting every raw OpenCode LLM record appended under the trace directory since the previous invocation, updating the token totals, cost, and call counts attributed to OpenCode-driven calls, the shared prompt-cache sample window, and the LLM-status history accordingly. Log files that first appear between invocations are ingested from their beginning, and within each file no log byte is ingested more than once across successive invocations; if a given file's length has shrunk below the position ingested so far, ingestion of that file resumes from its beginning so the aggregates reflect the file's current content. Blank-only lines and lines that do not decode as JSON are skipped without altering any aggregate and without interrupting ingestion of subsequent lines; a malformed line never causes the call to raise. A log file disappearing or becoming unreadable mid-scan leaves the aggregates for remaining files intact and does not abort the scan. When the directory does not yet exist or no log file contains bytes beyond its previously ingested position, returns leaving every aggregate unchanged. The call is strictly read-only toward the log directory: it never creates, truncates, writes, or deletes any log file, and it mutates only the state's own aggregate fields and read cursors.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Let S denote the State instance and D = S.opencode_dir. **Path 1 Directory absent (Line 2-3):** If D does not exist (D.exists() is False), the method returns None immediately. No field of S is mutated; S._opencode_offsets is unchanged; no file I/O is performed. **Path 2 Directory present, normal completion:** If D exists, the method iterates over every file whose name matches '*.jsonl' in D, in lexicographic (sorted) order. For each such file P with name N: (a) If P.stat() raises OSError, P is skipped entirely; S._opencode_offsets is not modified for N. (b) Let sz = P.stat().st_size and prev_off = S._opencode_offsets.get(N, 0). If sz < prev_off (truncation or replacement), the effective read offset is reset to 0. If sz equals the effective offset, P is skipped (no new data). (c) Otherwise the file is opened in text mode (UTF-8), seeked to the effective offset, and every line from that position to the current end-of-file is examined: - Blank or whitespace-only lines are silently discarded. - Lines for which json.loads raises any Exception are silently discarded. - Every remaining line is decoded to a JSON object rec, and S._ingest_opencode(rec, N) is called exactly once, in file order. By the contract of _ingest_opencode, this call does not raise and updates S's OpenCode-attributed aggregates (token counts, cost, call count, prompt-cache window, LLM-status history, and correlat…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-006 — `dashboard-py--_fmt_tokens`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/_fmt_tokens.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/_fmt_tokens.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--_fmt_tokens.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--_fmt_tokens.py)
- 触发/冲突：For n = 1_000_000_000 (one billion), the spec requires scaling by the largest magnitude unit keeping the scaled value >= 1 — billion (yielding '1.00B') — but the implementation's unit ladder caps at millions, so it returns '1000.00M'.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：n is either a non-negative integer token count, or None, indicating the count is unavailable.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a non-empty compact human-readable string representing n. When n is None, returns a placeholder marker denoting an unavailable count. When n is a number below one thousand, returns the exact decimal representation of n. Otherwise n is scaled by the largest magnitude unit (thousand, million, ...) that keeps the scaled value at or above 1, and the result is rendered as a fixed-precision decimal string suffixed with that unit's marker. The returned string never misrepresents the magnitude of n, and equal inputs always produce equal outputs. Pure formatting: accesses no external state and performs no filesystem access.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` and raises no exceptions. Exactly one of the following cases holds: (1) If n is None, then result == "" (U+2014 em dash). (2) If n is an integer and n >= 1_000_000, then result == f"{n/1_000_000:.2f}M", i.e., the decimal representation of n/1_000_000 rounded to exactly 2 decimal places followed by the character 'M'. (3) If n is an integer and 1_000 <= n < 1_000_000, then result == f"{n/1_000:.1f}K", i.e., the decimal representation of n/1_000 rounded to exactly 1 decimal place followed by the character 'K'. (4) If n is an integer and 0 <= n < 1_000, then result == str(n), the base-10 decimal string of n with no leading zeros. Formally: n ( {None}), result str ((n = None result = "") (n n 10 result = format(n/10, '.2f') + 'M') (n 10 n < 10 result = format(n/10, '.1f') + 'K') (n n < 10 result = str(n))). The function is pure: no global state is modified, no I/O occurs, and no exceptions are raised under the given pre-condition.

</details>

- 成因复核：反例不满足真实输入域、忽略调用方保证，或原始推导误读了代码/控制流。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：d is a possibly nested mapping decoded from a trace record; keys is a non-empty sequence naming a path of nested fields from outermost to innermost. The mapping is decoded external data, so any intermediate level may be absent, non-mapping, or missing the next key.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the value reached by following the key path through d from outermost to innermost, when every intermediate level along the path is a mapping containing the next key. Returns None when the path cannot be completed — that is, when any intermediate level reached before consuming all keys is not a mapping, or when the next key is absent from the current mapping. A key present at an intermediate level whose associated value is None is indistinguishable in the result from an absent key: both yield None. The call never raises regardless of the shape or contents of d or keys, and d (including any nested values) is never mutated. When keys is empty, the return value is d itself.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates and returns a value `result`. Let n = len(keys) (n 1 by pre-condition). Define the traversal sequence v_0, v_1, ..., v_n inductively: v_0 = d; for each i in 0..n-1, if isinstance(v_i, dict) then v_{i+1} = v_i.get(keys[i]) (yielding None when keys[i] is absent), otherwise the function short-circuits. Then: (1) If there exists an index i with 0 i < n such that v_i is not an instance of dict (including the case where a prior .get() returned None or a non-dict value), the function returns None at that iteration. (2) If for every i in 0..n-1, v_i is an instance of dict, the loop completes and the function returns v_n, which equals the value stored at the nested path d[keys[0]][keys[1]]...[keys[n-1]] if every key is present at its respective level, or None if any key is absent at its level (since dict.get defaults to None). Formally: result = None if i [0,n): isinstance(v_i, dict); otherwise result = v_n where v_n = (((d.get(keys[0])).get(keys[1]))...).get(keys[n-1]). No mutation of d or any nested sub-mapping occurs. No exception is raised provided every element of keys is hashable; if an unhashable key is encountered, a TypeError propagates. The input d and keys are unchanged.

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-008 — `dashboard-py--_llm_status_style`

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：code is the raw result code recorded for one LLM call and status is the raw status string recorded for the same call. Either or both may be absent (None), and when present they may be non-string values. No further guarantees are made about their values.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a two-element tuple (color, label) classifying the recorded call's outcome for display, where color is the display color of the outcome class and label is the display string. It is a total function: it never raises for any combination of inputs, including absent values and non-string values. The classification is deterministic — identical inputs always yield the same tuple — and partitions all calls into exactly three outcome classes. Success class (success color): a call whose recorded outcome indicates that the call completed at the protocol level — namely, a call whose recorded result code is the numeric HTTP status for a successful response (200), or whose recorded status string names a completed spec-verification outcome, covering both a passing verdict and a verdict reporting a detected spec violation. All success-class members share one single canonical success label. Warning class (warning color): a call that failed at the format/protocol level — namely, a call whose recorded status string names a response-format failure, whose recorded result code belongs to the HTTP client-error status class, or whose recorded result code is the dedicated format-failure sentinel. Error class (error color): every remaining outcome. A call belongs to the success class if and only if its returned label is the canonical success label. For warning-class and error-class members, th…
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function always terminates normally and returns a 2-tuple (color, display) where color {"green", "yellow", "red"} and display is a string. Let text str(code or status or "?") using Python's short-circuit `or` semantics (i.e., the first truthy operand among code, status, "?", or the last operand "?" if all are falsy). Then exactly one of the following holds: 1. (text == "200") (status {"success", "mismatch"}) return value is ("green", "200"). 2. (condition 1) (status == "format_error" text.startswith("4") text == "FMT") return value is ("yellow", text). 3. (condition 1) (condition 2) return value is ("red", text). No exception is raised: str() is total over all Python objects, the `or` chain is defined for any operands, membership testing against a tuple is total, and str.startswith is safe since text is guaranteed to be of type str. The parameters code and status are not mutated. The returned display string in the green branch is always the literal "200" regardless of the original code/status values; in the yellow and red branches it equals text as computed on line 2.

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-009 — `dashboard-py--_parse_iso`

- 结论：**契约待确认**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/_parse_iso.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/_parse_iso.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--_parse_iso.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--_parse_iso.py)
- 触发/冲突：Calling _parse_iso with an ISO timestamp lacking a UTC offset alongside one carrying an explicit offset yields a naive and an aware datetime, and comparing them (e.g., min/max) raises TypeError, violating the spec's mutual order-comparability guarantee.
- 成因复核：实现与生成 SPEC 确有差异，但仓库现有证据不足以决定产品应采用哪种行为。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：ts is the value of a timestamp field taken from a decoded event or trace record. It may be None, an empty string, or an arbitrary string not conforming to any particular format. No further guarantees are made about its value.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Total parsing function that never raises for any input. Returns a datetime representing ts when ts is a non-empty string conforming to ISO-8601 date-time syntax — date and time components with optional fractional seconds and optional UTC offset — and returns None in all other cases, including absent (None), empty, and malformed input. A trailing 'Z' UTC designator is accepted as equivalent to an explicit zero UTC offset, so a timestamp written with 'Z' parses to the same instant as the identical timestamp written with the explicit zero offset. All datetimes returned across separate calls are mutually order-comparable, so callers can min- and max-compare parsed timestamps from different records to maintain monotonic first/last event bounds, and can safely use a returned value as a fallback timestamp. The function performs no filesystem access, no I/O, and mutates no state.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function _parse_iso returns a value R such that exactly one of the following holds: 1. If ts is None or ts == "" (i.e., ts is falsy), then R is None. 2. If ts is a non-empty string: Let ts' = (ts[:-1] + "+00:00") if ts.endswith("Z") else ts. (a) If datetime.fromisoformat(ts') succeeds without raising, then R is the datetime.datetime object produced by that call, representing the parsed timestamp. (b) If datetime.fromisoformat(ts') raises any Exception (ValueError, TypeError, etc.), then R is None. Formal logic: (ts is None ts == "") R = None (ts is a non-empty string ts.endswith("Z")) let ts' = ts[:-1]+"+00:00" in (fromisoformat(ts') succeeds R = fromisoformat(ts')) (fromisoformat(ts') raises R = None) (ts is a non-empty string ts.endswith("Z")) (fromisoformat(ts) succeeds R = fromisoformat(ts)) (fromisoformat(ts) raises R = None) Additional guarantees: - The function never propagates an exception to the caller; all exceptions from datetime.fromisoformat are caught by the bare 'except Exception' clause. - No external state is mutated; the local rebinding of ts on line 5 has no side effect outside the function. - R {None} {d : d is an instance of datetime.datetime}. - The input ts is not modified (strings are immutable in Python; the local rebinding does not affect the caller's reference).

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-010 — `dashboard-py--_strip_star`

- 结论：**推理误判**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/_strip_star.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/_strip_star.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--_strip_star.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--_strip_star.py)
- 触发/冲突：Passing a dict that carries a non-string (integer) key such as {1: 'value'} enters the mapping path and evaluates str.startswith() on that key, raising AttributeError, which violates the specification's requirement that the call never raises regardless of d's contents.
- 成因复核：反例不满足真实输入域、忽略调用方保证，或原始推导误读了代码/控制流。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：d is a value decoded from a raw OpenCode trace record or one of its nested usage payloads; when d is a mapping, its keys are strings that may or may not carry the trace plugin's version-specific leading key prefix; d may also be a non-mapping value.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：When d is a mapping, returns a new mapping containing exactly the same entries and values as d, except that every key carrying the version-specific leading prefix appears under its plain-named equivalent with that prefix removed, so a field addressed by its prefixed name resolves to the same value as its plain-named equivalent; keys without the prefix pass through unchanged. When d is not a mapping, it is returned unchanged. The input mapping is never mutated, and the call never raises regardless of d's shape or contents.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Let r denote the return value of _strip_star(d). The post-condition is a disjunction over two mutually exclusive paths: 1. Non-mapping path (not isinstance(d, dict)): r is d (object identity); no new object is created; no exception is raised. 2. Mapping path (isinstance(d, dict)): r is a newly constructed dict (r is not d) such that: (a) For every key k in d with associated value v: - If k.startswith('*') is True, then the key k[1:] (i.e., k with its first character removed) maps to v in r. - If k.startswith('*') is False, then the key k maps to v in r. (b) The key set of r equals { (k[1:] if k.startswith('*') else k) for k in d.keys() }. (c) The original dict d is not mutated; its keys, values, and insertion order remain unchanged. (d) Values are shallow-copied by reference; no recursive stripping is applied to nested structures. (e) If two distinct keys in d (e.g., '*x' and 'x') map to the same stripped key, the later key in d's iteration order determines the value in r (standard dict-comprehension overwrite semantics). (f) No exception is raised, given the pre-condition guarantees all keys are strings. Formally: (isinstance(d, dict) r d) (isinstance(d, dict) r d k keys(d): let k' = k[1:] if k[0]=='*' else k in (k' keys(r) r[k'] == d[k]) keys(r) == {k[1:] if k[0]=='*' else k | k keys(d)} keys(d) unchanged values(d) unchanged)

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-012 — `dashboard-py--build_layout`

- 结论：**推理误判**；内置 validator：`not_confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/build_layout.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/build_layout.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--build_layout.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--build_layout.py)
- 触发/冲突：build_layout hardcodes tokens_h = 3 + 6 for the token/cost region, allegedly assuming exactly two source categories plus one grand-total row, but render_tokens unconditionally emits exactly those three rows (it iterates no state-derived category collection), so across seven probed state shapes — empty, single-source, both-source, all usage shapes, and states holding an extra source aggregate — the allocated height 9 always equals both the rendered panel height and the spec-derived rows+6 height, with no truncation or blank padding.
- 成因复核：validator 的复现阶段已否定该 claim；原始 MISMATCH 是代码、控制流或可达性读取错误。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：state is a fully initialized dashboard State object whose aggregates reflect all trace data ingested so far: per-stage progress, token/cost totals, prompt-cache hit statistics, verification verdict counts, bug-validation outcome counts, the recent-event log, and current LLM-call status. state may be concurrently updated by the ingestion loop after the call, so the function must treat it as read-only.

Regions whose content is shorter than their allocation are vertically centered, with spare space distributed evenly above and below the content. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-013 — `dashboard-py--render_recent`

- 结论：**推理误判**；内置 validator：`not_confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/dashboard-py/render_recent.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/dashboard-py/render_recent.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/dashboard-py--render_recent.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_dashboard-py--render_recent.py)
- 触发/冲突：render_recent allegedly iterates state.recent_events in natural storage order without reversal, so a chronologically stored (oldest-first) log would render oldest-first in violation of the spec's most-recent-first requirement; however, the log's only writer State._push_recent appendlefts every ingested event, so the live log is always maintained newest-first and every reachable state — chronological ingestion, timestamp skew, two-wave incremental refresh, empty log, single event, and 45-event capacity overflow — renders most-recently-ingested-first as the spec requires.
- 成因复核：validator 的复现阶段已否定该 claim；原始 MISMATCH 是代码、控制流或可达性读取错误。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：state is a fully initialized dashboard State object whose recent-event log reflects the most recently ingested trace events; each event entry records an ingestion time-of-day string, a pipeline stage name, a status string, and a human-readable summary. state may be concurrently updated by the ingestion loop after the call, so the function must treat state as read-only.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a bordered panel renderable titled 'Recent Events' containing a table that lists the most recently ingested trace events from state's recent-event log in most-recent-first order, with exactly one row per event. Every row displays the same four fields of its event: ingestion time, pipeline stage, status, and summary. Each row's status field is rendered in a color determined by a fixed status classification: a status denoting successful completion renders in the success color; each status denoting a distinct failure category renders in its own distinct failure color; and any status outside the recognized classification falls back to a neutral default color. When the event log is empty the function still returns the panel (with a header-only table) and does not raise. The function is pure rendering: it does not mutate any field of state and performs no filesystem access.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a rich Panel object P such that: (1) P.title == "Recent Events" and P.border_style == "cyan"; (2) P.renderable is a rich Table T with show_header=True, header_style="bold", expand=True, pad_edge=False, and exactly four columns named "time", "stage", "status", "summary" in that order with their respective styles (dim/cyan/default/default) and no_wrap=True on all, with overflow="ellipsis" on the summary column; (3) Let snapshot = list(state.recent_events) evaluated at entry. T contains exactly len(snapshot) rows, one per event tuple (when, stage, status, summary) in snapshot order; (4) For each row i corresponding to event (when_i, stage_i, status_i, summary_i): the first cell is when_i styled dim, the second cell is stage_i styled cyan, the third cell is the Rich markup string f"[{color_i}]{status_i}[/]" where color_i is "green" if status_i=="success", "yellow" if status_i=="mismatch", "red" if status_i=="error", "magenta" if status_i=="format_error", and "white" otherwise, and the fourth cell is summary_i; (5) state is not mutated: all attributes of state, including state.recent_events, retain their pre-call values and identity (read-only guarantee); (6) No exception is raised provided state.recent_events is iterable and each element is a 4-tuple of strings; if state.recent_events is not iterable a TypeError propagates, and if any element is not unpackable…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-014 — `src--call_graph_edges-py--_clean_label`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/call_graph_edges-py/_clean_label.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/call_graph_edges-py/_clean_label.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--call_graph_edges-py--_clean_label.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--call_graph_edges-py--_clean_label.py)
- 触发/冲突：Calling the public entry point normalize_fqn_label (src.call_graph_edges) with label "';'" reaches _clean_label, where rstrip(';') is a no-op because the last char is a quote and the subsequent quote-pair removal exposes ';', so the function returns ';' with a trailing semicolon, violating spec guarantee (2) which requires no trailing ';' character.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：value is the raw text of one endpoint-name field (or one element of a string-array field) of a supplemental edge mapping. Its content is unverified: it may be empty, whitespace-only, carry surrounding whitespace, end in one or more ';' characters, be enclosed in one pair of matching surrounding quote characters, or already be a clean FM-Agent FQN. Where a caller requires a specific type it has been type-checked by that caller before this call; otherwise the function's contract is defined over the string form of value.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the label-cleaned form of value. Cleaning proceeds as follows: value is first coerced to its string form; surrounding whitespace is stripped; then all trailing ';' characters are removed and any surrounding whitespace newly exposed by that removal is stripped; then at most one pair of matching surrounding quote characters — present only when the remaining text has length at least 2 and its first and last characters are the same single or double quote — is removed, and any surrounding whitespace exposed by that removal is stripped as well. All characters other than these decorations are preserved in their original relative order. Guarantees: (1) the returned string contains no leading or trailing whitespace; (2) it contains no trailing ';' character; (3) it is empty if and only if cleaning reduces value's string form to no content; (4) when non-empty, it never retains a matching surrounding quote pair; (5) the result depends only on value's string form, so cleaning is deterministic. The function performs no I/O, raises no exception for string input, and does not modify value.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` such that: (1) `result` is of type `str`. (2) `result` contains no leading or trailing whitespace (i.e., `result == result.strip()`). (3) Let `t1 = str(value).strip()`. If `t1 == ""`, then `result == ""`. (4) Otherwise, let `t2 = t1.rstrip(";").strip()`. If `len(t2) >= 2` and `t2[0] == t2[-1]` and `t2[0] in {"'", '"'}`, then `result == t2[1:-1].strip()`; otherwise `result == t2.strip()`. (5) In all cases, `result` has no trailing semicolons that were present in the original stripped input, and any single layer of matching surrounding single-quote or double-quote characters (present after semicolon removal) has been removed. (6) No exception is raised assuming `str(value)` is well-defined (guaranteed by the pre-condition). Formally: value satisfying the pre-condition, ! result str such that result = _clean_label(value) result == result.strip() (str(value).strip() == "" result == "") (str(value).strip() != "" let t2 = str(value).strip().rstrip(";").strip() in ((len(t2) >= 2 t2[0] == t2[-1] t2[0] {chr(39), chr(34)}) result == t2[1:-1].strip()) ((len(t2) >= 2 t2[0] == t2[-1] t2[0] {chr(39), chr(34)}) result == t2.strip())).

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-015 — `src--call_graph_edges-py--_is_path_function_label`

- 结论：**推理误判**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/call_graph_edges-py/_is_path_function_label.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/call_graph_edges-py/_is_path_function_label.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--call_graph_edges-py--_is_path_function_label.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--call_graph_edges-py--_is_path_function_label.py)
- 触发/冲突：For a label whose prefix before the last '::' ends with a trailing slash (e.g. "a/.b/::func"), PurePosixPath(path).name strips the trailing slash and sees '.b' containing '.', so _is_path_function_label returns True although the specification requires False because the last '/'-separated component of the prefix is the empty string.
- 成因复核：反例不满足真实输入域、忽略调用方保证，或原始推导误读了代码/控制流。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：label is a label-cleaned endpoint-name string from a supplemental edge mapping: with all decoration characters (surrounding whitespace, trailing ';' characters, matching surrounding quotes) already removed, it is either (a) an FM-Agent FQN — a sequence of '::'-separated name components carrying no path separators and no file-extension dot in its file-position component — or (b) a path-style label of the form "dir/file.ext::func" in which a path component precedes a function component, the two separated by '::'. label is of type str.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True exactly when label has the path-style textual form, which holds if and only if all three of the following are true: label contains at least one '::' separator; the prefix before the last '::' separator contains at least one '/' path-separator character; and the last '/'-separated path component of that prefix contains at least one '.' character. Returns False for every other label, including every label already in FQN form and every label containing no '::' separator at all. The decision depends only on the textual structure of label — the same label always yields the same result — and label is neither modified nor consulted by any I/O; the function performs no I/O and raises no exception for any string input.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a bool and does not mutate label (str is immutable). No exception is raised under the given pre-condition. Formally, let R denote the return value. Then: R = True if and only if ('::' label) AND, letting (path, _func) = label.rsplit('::', 1), ('/' path) AND ('.' PurePosixPath(path).name). Otherwise R = False. Equivalently, under the stated pre-condition: R = True exactly when label is a path-style label of the form 'dir/file.ext::func' (case b), and R = False exactly when label is an FM-Agent FQN (case a), since FQN components carry no '/' and no file-extension dot. In all paths label remains unchanged, no global or mutable state is modified, and the only observable effect is the returned boolean. In logical notation: label str satisfying the pre-condition, _is_path_function_label(label) = ('::' in label path, func: label.rsplit('::',1) = (path, func) '/' in path '.' in PurePosixPath(path).name).

</details>

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/cli_backend-py/messages_to_prompt.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/cli_backend-py/messages_to_prompt.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--cli_backend-py--messages_to_prompt.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--cli_backend-py--messages_to_prompt.py)
- 触发/冲突：A message whose non-string content is a list of text blocks (e.g. [{'text': 'Hello'}, {'text': 'World'}]) is flattened with '\n'.join, inserting a newline between adjacent block texts where the specification requires direct concatenation with no separator.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：messages is a non-empty list of well-formed chat message dicts. Each dict carries a 'role' field whose value is a string, and a 'content' field whose value is either a string or a list of text-block dicts in which each block exposes a string-valued 'text' field.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a single string that renders every message in messages in input order, with no message dropped, reordered, or altered. Each message renders as its role label in upper case, followed immediately by a colon and a newline, followed by the rendering of that message's content. Renderings of adjacent messages are separated by exactly one blank line. A string content value appears verbatim, character for character. A non-string content value is flattened to the concatenation of its text blocks' 'text' field values taken in original list order, so every block's text contributes to the result in the order the blocks appear. The returned string therefore contains the full content of the entire conversation, and is suitable for delivery as the prompt to an agent subprocess. The function never mutates messages, any of its element dicts, or any text block; it is deterministic for a given messages value, and raises no exception for any well-formed input satisfying the pre-condition.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates normally and returns a string `result`. No exceptions are raised under the given pre-condition. Formally: Let n = len(messages) 1. For each index i in [0, n), define segment_i as follows: let role_i = messages[i]['role'] (a string, guaranteed by pre-condition) and content_i = messages[i]['content']. If isinstance(content_i, str), then processed_i = content_i. This text represents the complete textual output produced by the local agent CLI backend in response to the constructed prompt. - metadata is the empty dict {}. - The constructed prompt delivered to the agent is the fixed instruction header ('Answer the following conversation directly. Preserve any requested output tags exactly and do not add unrelated commentary.\n\n') concatenated with the output of messages_to_prompt(messages), which renders every message in order without mutation. - The subprocess was invoked in the directory os.path.abspath(os.getcwd()) with a wall-clock timeout of 1800 seconds. 2. RuntimeError (subprocess exits with returncode != 0): A RuntimeError is raised whose message string matches the pattern f"{command.backend} exited with code {result.returncode}: {output}" where output is the last 4000 characters (or fewer, if total output is shorter) of the combined stdout/stderr text (or the empty string if stdout is None).…

The returned path is scoped to the current user — derived from the numeric user id when the platform provides one, otherwise from the user's login name — so that the private backup locations of different users on the same machine are distinct and their backups can never collide. The user-scoped component of the returned path contains only filesystem-safe characters. The returned path lies under the system temporary directory. The result is deterministic: every call for the same user on the same machine returns the same path, independent of the time of the call and of the process working directory. (4) If the platform provides os.getuid (i.e., hasattr(os, 'getuid') is True), the unsanitized user_component is the string f"uid-{os.getuid()}"; otherwise, user_component is the first truthy value among os.environ.get("USERNAME"), os.environ.get("USER"), and the literal "user". (5) `safe` is derived from user_component by replacing every maximal run of characters outside [A-Za-z0-9._-] with a single underscore, then stripping leading/trailing occurrences of '.', '_', and '-'; if the result is the empty string, `safe` defaults to "user". (6) Under the stated pre-condition (a user identity is available via uid or environment), the function completes normally without raising an exception and the returned Path is well-formed. Formally: safe str: len(safe) > 0 ( c safe: c {A-Z, a-z…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-024 — `src--configure_llm-py--_quote_toml_string`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/configure_llm-py/_quote_toml_string.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/configure_llm-py/_quote_toml_string.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--configure_llm-py--_quote_toml_string.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--configure_llm-py--_quote_toml_string.py)
- 触发/冲突：A [llm] setting value containing the DEL control character U+007F is emitted unescaped by json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False), producing a TOML basic string that TOML v1.0.0 requires to be escaped, so the generated fm-agent.toml document is rejected by the TOML parser and the value cannot round-trip.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：value is one of the string values of updates to be written into the [llm] section of the FM-Agent project TOML configuration document; per the caller's contract, no secret material appears among those values.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a double-quoted TOML basic-string literal. When the returned literal is parsed as TOML, it yields exactly value, so every setting value substituted through this literal round-trips unchanged. The returned text is a valid TOML basic-string literal for every input string: every character that TOML requires to be escaped inside a basic string — the double quote, the backslash, and every control character other than tab — appears only in escaped form, and no raw character appears in a way that invalidates the literal or alters the decoded value; non-ASCII characters neither invalidate the literal nor alter the decoded value. Substituting any [llm] setting with the returned literal therefore keeps the document valid TOML. The result depends only on value; the function modifies no file and produces no observable state change.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` such that: (1) `result` is a valid TOML basic string literal representing `value`; specifically, `result` begins and ends with a double-quote character ('"'), and all characters requiring escaping within a TOML basic string (backslash, double-quote, and control characters U+0000U+001F) are escaped using JSON-compatible escape sequences (e.g., \\, \", \n, \t, \uXXXX). (2) Because `ensure_ascii=False` is passed to `json.dumps`, any non-ASCII characters present in `value` appear verbatim (as their original Unicode code points) in `result` rather than being encoded as \uXXXX sequences. (3) Round-trip fidelity holds: parsing `result` as a TOML basic string (or equivalently as a JSON string) yields exactly `value`, i.e., `toml.loads('k=' + result)['k'] == value`. (4) No exception is raised for any input satisfying the pre-condition (i.e., any `str` instance); the function terminates normally. (5) No secret material is introduced, transformed, or leaked by this operation; the function is a pure, deterministic string-formatting transformation with no side effects, no I/O, and no mutation of external state. Formally: value str, ! result str such that result = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False) result[0] == '"' result[-1] == '"' json.loads(result) == value result is a syntactically valid TOML basic string.

</details>

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/configure_llm-py/backup_file.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/configure_llm-py/backup_file.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--configure_llm-py--backup_file.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--configure_llm-py--backup_file.py)
- 触发/冲突：Calling backup_file() with private=False on a file named .env places the backup alongside the original file in its (group/world-accessible) directory instead of the owner-only private backup location, because the placement decision checks only the private flag.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：path designates a configuration or secret file the caller intends to modify; its pre-modification content must be preserved. The file may or may not exist. private states whether the file's content is secret material. When now is given, it fixes the timestamp that distinguishes this backup in its name; when absent, the current local time is used.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：When the file at path does not exist, returns None and creates nothing. When the file exists, creates a backup whose content is byte-identical to the content of path immediately before the call, and returns the backup file's path. The backup destination is freshly reserved so that no call can overwrite an earlier backup, even under rapid or concurrent invocations: repeated calls within the same second for the same file yield distinct backup paths rather than clobbering one another. When the content is not secret, the backup is placed alongside the original file in its directory. When the content is secret — because the caller flagged private, or because the file's conventional name marks it as the project .env secret file — the backup is placed in a private backup location dedicated to the owning user, and both that location and the backup file grant access only to the owning user. The original file at path is never modified. If the backup copy cannot be completed, no partial backup file is left behind at the reserved destination and the underlying error propagates to the caller.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates in one of three outcomes: 1. EARLY RETURN (path does not exist): If path.exists() was False at entry, the function returns None. No filesystem modifications are made. The variable `now` is not reassigned. 2. SUCCESSFUL BACKUP (path exists and copy succeeds): The function returns a Path object `backup` such that: (a) `backup` designates a newly created file whose content is byte-identical to the content of `path` at the moment of copying (shutil.copy2 semantics preserve content and metadata timestamps). (b) The original file at `path` is unmodified in content, permissions, and metadata. (c) The backup filename is of the form `{path.name}.bak.{suffix}` where suffix = now.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S'), using the caller-supplied `now` if not None, otherwise datetime.now() at the time of the call. If that name was already taken, a numeric disambiguator `.{attempt}` (attempt 1) is appended. (d) If private is True: the backup resides under _private_backup_root(); that directory exists with mode 0o700 (created with parents if absent); the backup file has mode 0o600; the filename is prefixed with a slug derived from path.parent.resolve() with os.sep replaced by '_' (or 'project' if the slug would be empty), followed by '__'. (e) If private is False: the backup resides in the same directory as `path` (path.with_name(...)); the file mode is 0o600 if path.name == '.env…

</details>

If the provider entry contains an 'options' key whose value is not a dict (and is not None), raises ConfigWizardError indicating options must be an object. 4. If the provider entry contains a 'models' key whose value is not a dict (and is not None), raises ConfigWizardError indicating models must be an object. 5. If the models sub-dict contains `config.model_id` mapped to a value that is not a dict (and is not None), raises ConfigWizardError indicating the model entry must be an object. 6. If `adapter_for_api_style(config.api_style)` raises ConfigWizardError (only reachable if an unsupported style bypassed earlier validation, contradicting the stated pre-condition), that exception propagates. In all exception paths, neither `existing` nor `config` is mutated (the function operates on a deep copy from line 7 onward). **Normal return path:** Returns a dict `merged` such that: - `merged` is a deep copy of `existing`; the original `exis…

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

(3) Every other line of text is retained byte-for-byte, with one exception: assignment lines whose variable names belong to the deprecated set of legacy LLM override environment variables are removed outright — the caller has already retained their values in the TOML configuration before this call. (4) When a new secret-key assignment is appended and the retained content is non-empty, a blank line separates it from the preceding content; when text is empty, the result consists of comment lines identifying the file as the gitignored secret store for the LLM API key, followed by that single assignment. (5) The returned text is valid .env content: the secret key appears in no assignment other than those holding api_key, and no secret material other than api_key is introduced.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` such that: 1. **Exactly one ENV_SECRET_KEY assignment exists**: `result` contains exactly one line assigning `ENV_SECRET_KEY` to `api_key`. If the original `text` contained a line with key `ENV_SECRET_KEY`, that line is replaced in-place (preserving any leading whitespace and 'export ' prefix) with `{export_prefix}{ENV_SECRET_KEY}={api_key}\n`. If no such line existed, the assignment `{ENV_SECRET_KEY}={api_key}\n` is appended at the end. 2. **Legacy keys removed**: Every line from `text` whose parsed key (after stripping whitespace and optional 'export ' prefix) is in `ENV_LEGACY_LLM_KEYS` is omitted from `result`. 3. **All other lines preserved in order**: Every line from `text` that is blank, a comment (starts with '#' after optional leading whitespace), a KEY=VALUE line whose key is neither `ENV_SECRET_KEY` nor in `ENV_LEGACY_LLM_KEYS`, or a line without '=' separator, appears in `result` unchanged and in its original relative order. 4. **Append formatting when key was absent**: If `ENV_SECRET_KEY` was not found in `text` (i.e., `key_written` remained False): - If `result` would otherwise be empty, two comment header lines (`# fm-agent secrets gitignored, do not commit.\n` and `# Only the LLM API key belongs here.\n`) are prepended before the assignment. - If `result` is non-empty and its last line is non-blank, a blank line `\n` is in…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-033 — `src--configure_llm-py--validate_base_url`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/configure_llm-py/validate_base_url.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/configure_llm-py/validate_base_url.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--configure_llm-py--validate_base_url.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--configure_llm-py--validate_base_url.py)
- 触发/冲突：validate_base_url checks urlparse(url).netloc (truthy ':8080') instead of the host part, so 'http://:8080' — an http URL with an empty host — is silently accepted (returns None) instead of raising ConfigWizardError.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：url is a base URL candidate string taken unchanged from the caller, with leading and trailing whitespace already removed: either the base URL field of the wizard answer set or a candidate value for the base URL setting under validation. There is no guarantee that url is well-formed; the function exists to decide that.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns normally (returning None) only when url is an absolute URL that satisfies both of the following: its scheme component is exactly http or https, and its host part is non-empty. Raises ConfigWizardError instead of returning for every url that fails either requirement — a string that is empty, relative, schemeless, carries any scheme other than http or https, or carries an http or https scheme but an empty host part. The exception message identifies the rejected value. The function performs no I/O, writes no file, prints nothing, and modifies no state; its outcome depends only on url.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Exactly one of the following two outcomes holds: 1. Normal return: The function returns None. This occurs if and only if urlparse(url).scheme {"http", "https"} AND urlparse(url).netloc is a non-empty (truthy) string. In this case the url has been confirmed to be a syntactically valid absolute HTTP or HTTPS URL with a network-location component. No exception is raised. The input string url is unchanged (Python str immutability). No observable global or caller-visible state is mutated; the local variable `parsed` ceases to exist. 2. Exceptional exit: A ConfigWizardError is raised and propagates to the caller. This occurs if and only if urlparse(url).scheme {"http", "https"} OR urlparse(url).netloc is falsy (empty string). The exception message is exactly f"Base URL must be an absolute http(s) URL, got: {url!r}". The function does not return a value. The input string url is unchanged. Formally, letting P = urlparse(url): (return_value = None P.scheme {"http","https"} P.netloc "") XOR (raises ConfigWizardError(msg) msg = f"Base URL must be an absolute http(s) URL, got: {url!r}" (P.scheme {"http","https"} P.netloc = "")) In both paths the parameter url is not mutated, no side-effects on external state occur, and no other exception type is intentionally raised by this code block.

</details>

- 触发/冲突：Passing merged_opencode containing a non-JSON-serializable object() value (or, symmetrically, malformed TOML as updated_toml) makes validate_generated_state propagate the raw TypeError/TOMLDecodeError from its unwrapped json.dumps/tomllib.loads checks instead of raising the spec-required ConfigWizardError naming the violated consistency requirement.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：The generated configuration state is fully constructed: config is the fully collected wizard answer set including the secret API key, merged_opencode is the merged OpenCode configuration produced for this run, updated_toml is the generated project TOML text in which no secret material appears, and opencode_secret_path is the private per-provider key file path where the API key is stored.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Performs the post-write self-check that the generated configuration state is mutually consistent, and returns normally only when every one of the following holds: the answer set itself conforms to the wizard input grammar; merged_opencode is JSON-serializable; updated_toml is well-formed TOML; merged_opencode contains a provider entry for exactly the provider selected in config; that entry declares the adapter package corresponding to the API protocol family selected in config; that entry references the API key through opencode_secret_path rather than inlining the key itself; and that entry declares the model selected in config. Raises ConfigWizardError naming the violated consistency requirement when any single check fails. Writes no file and modifies no state.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function validate_generated_state is a pure validation routine that modifies no state, writes no files, and mutates none of its arguments. It terminates in exactly one of the following outcomes: 1. NORMAL RETURN (returns None): All of the following hold simultaneously: (a) validate_input(config) returned normally, confirming every field of config conforms to the configuration grammar. (b) merged_opencode is JSON-serializable (json.dumps succeeded without raising TypeError or ValueError). (c) updated_toml is syntactically valid TOML (tomllib.loads succeeded without raising tomllib.TOMLDecodeError). (d) merged_opencode["provider"] is a dict and contains the key config.provider_id. (e) merged_opencode["provider"][config.provider_id] is a dict. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-035 — `src--domain_knowledge-py--stage_domain_knowledge_files`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/domain_knowledge-py/stage_domain_knowledge_files.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/domain_knowledge-py/stage_domain_knowledge_files.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--domain_knowledge-py--stage_domain_knowledge_files.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--domain_knowledge-py--stage_domain_knowledge_files.py)
- 触发/冲突：The staging directory is swapped via shutil.rmtree(target_dir) followed by os.replace(tmp_dir, target_dir), leaving a window in which the staging directory does not exist at all, so observers see neither the complete previous nor the complete new file set, violating the spec's atomic-replacement guarantee.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：proj_dir is the path of an existing project root directory; relative knowledge-path entries are resolved against it, falling back to the process working directory. work_dir is the FM-Agent working directory path (<proj_dir>/fm_agent) inside which the user-knowledge staging directory may be created or atomically replaced. markdown_paths is either falsy or a possibly nested collection of non-empty string entries each naming a candidate knowledge file; entries may be absolute, user-home-relative, or relative to either resolution base.

When markdown_paths is falsy the function performs no filesystem mutation at all and returns the previously staged Markdown files unchanged ([] if the staging directory does not exist). When markdown_paths is non-empty, after return the staging directory contains exactly one copy per distinct requested source file (requested entries resolving to the same canonical file count once), one staging manifest in JSON form holding a 'files' array with one entry per staged file mapping that file's absolute source path to its project-relative staged path, and nothing else. The replacement of the staging directory is atomic: any observer sees either the complete previous file set or the complete new file set, never a mix. Staged file names are mutually distinct across the directory, filesystem-safe, and carry the source file's extension lower-cased. Raises ValueError, before staging any file, when any requested entry resolves to a path that does not exist, is not a regular file, or has a non-Markdown extension; a raised validation error leaves no partially staged directory behind.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates via one of the following mutually exclusive outcomes: **Path A Early return (markdown_paths is falsy):** No filesystem mutation occurs. The return value equals list_staged_domain_knowledge_relpaths(work_dir): a possibly-empty list of project-relative '/'-separated path strings (prefixed 'fm_agent/') naming the Markdown files currently present in the staging directory under work_dir, in ascending sorted order, excluding the manifest and non-Markdown files. If the staging directory does not exist, the return value is []. **Path B ValueError propagation:** If resolve_domain_knowledge_paths raises ValueError (because some entry in markdown_paths resolves to a nonexistent path, a non-regular file, or a non-Markdown extension), that ValueError propagates unchanged. No staging directory is created or modified; any pre-existing staging directory and its contents remain untouched. The temporary directory (target_dir + '.tmp') may or may not exist depending on when the exception occurred, but no atomic replacement has taken place. **Path C OS/IO exception during filesystem operations:** If any of os.makedirs, shutil.rmtree, shutil.copy2, open/json.dump, or os.replace raises OSError (or a subclass such as PermissionError, FileNotFoundError, IsADirectoryError), that exception propagates. The pre-existing staging directory at os.path.join(work_dir, USER_KNOWLEDGE_RE…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-036 — `src--entry_reasoning_pipeline-py--_count_mismatches`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。

- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

The caller makes no guarantee about the presence, readability, format, or schema of any individual file within the tree.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a non-negative integer equal to the number of files found at any depth under results_dir that (a) have a .json file-name suffix, (b) parse as JSON into an object, and (c) carry a top-level "verdict" field whose value is exactly the string "MISMATCH". Each qualifying file contributes exactly 1 to the total; files with any other suffix, files that cannot be read, files that fail JSON parsing, and JSON documents whose top-level verdict value differs from "MISMATCH" (or that lack a verdict field) contribute nothing. Returns 0 when results_dir does not exist or is empty. The function never raises for a missing directory or an unreadable or malformed file, and performs no writes anywhere.

Files that are not '.json', that raise OSError (including FileNotFoundError, PermissionError, IsADirectoryError) or ValueError (including json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError) during open/read/parse, or whose parsed JSON dict lacks a 'verdict' key or whose 'verdict' value differs from 'MISMATCH', do not contribute to the count. If `results_dir` does not exist, is not a directory, or is empty, os.walk yields no entries and the function returns 0. EXCEPTIONAL PATHS: (a) If any '.json' file contains syntactically valid JSON that is NOT a JSON object (e.g., a JSON array, string, number, boolean, or null), the call to `.get('verdict')` on the non-dict result raises an unhandled AttributeError that propagates to the caller. (b) If `results_dir` is not a valid path-like or string argument, os.walk may raise …

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。

- 触发/冲突：Calling _restrict_to_chains with an empty end_funcs list (e.g. call_graph={'a': ['b'], 'b': []}, entry_func='a', end_funcs=[]) takes the early-return branch and returns the original call_graph object by identity instead of a new mapping, violating the spec's unconditional 'Returns a new mapping; call_graph is never modified' guarantee.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：call_graph is a mapping from fully-qualified function names to collections of fully-qualified callee function names in which every key is reachable from entry_func over the edges of call_graph (the graph is rooted at entry_func). entry_func is a key of call_graph. end_funcs is a sequence of fully-qualified function names; entry-pipeline callers pass a non-empty sequence, and the function additionally tolerates an empty sequence.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a new mapping; call_graph is never modified. When end_funcs is empty, the returned mapping has the same keys and the same callee collections as call_graph. Otherwise the result contains exactly the functions lying on at least one call chain from entry_func to some member of end_funcs — precisely those functions from which some member of end_funcs is reachable — and every function outside all such chains is absent. Every edge present in the result connects two retained functions: for each retained function that is not itself a member of end_funcs, its callee collection consists of exactly those of its callees in call_graph that are retained, in the same relative order as in call_graph. Retained members of end_funcs are terminal stopping points: their callee collections in the result are empty, so no retained chain extends beyond a member of end_funcs. entry_func is included whenever at least one call chain from entry_func to a member of end_funcs exists, and the result is an empty mapping when no member of end_funcs is reachable from entry_func.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns without raising an exception under the given pre-condition. Two execution paths exist: **Path 1 (early return):** If `end_funcs` is falsy (empty sequence, None, etc.), the function returns the original `call_graph` object itself (identity: `result is call_graph`). No mutation of `call_graph` occurs. **Path 2 (normal flow, `end_funcs` is truthy):** Let `valid_ends = {ef | ef end_funcs ef keys(call_graph)}`. Let `on_chain` be the set of all nodes `n keys(call_graph)` such that there exists a directed path in `call_graph` from `n` to some node in `valid_ends` (equivalently, the set reachable from `valid_ends` via reverse-edge BFS over `call_graph`). The function returns a **new** dict `pruned` satisfying: 1. `set(pruned.keys()) = on_chain`. 2. For every `fqn pruned`: - If `fqn end_funcs` (terminal node): `pruned[fqn] = []` (empty list; outgoing edges are dropped). - Otherwise: `pruned[fqn] = [c for c in call_graph[fqn] if c on_chain]` (callee list filtered to on-chain nodes, preserving original order). 3. `call_graph` is not mutated. 4. `entry_func pruned` if and only if `entry_func` can reach at least one node in `valid_ends` via edges of `call_graph`. If no end_func is reachable from `entry_func` (or no end_func is a key of `call_graph`), then `on_chain = ` and `pruned = {}`. 5. Every value in `pruned` is a `list`. Every key in `pruned` is a key of the orig…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-039 — `src--entry_reasoning_pipeline-py--_trim_source_file`

- 结论：**契约待确认**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/entry_reasoning_pipeline-py/_trim_source_file.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/entry_reasoning_pipeline-py/_trim_source_file.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--entry_reasoning_pipeline-py--_trim_source_file.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--entry_reasoning_pipeline-py--_trim_source_file.py)
- 触发/冲突：Trimming a supported source file whose line endings differ from the platform default (e.g., a CRLF file on Linux) rewrites it via open(filepath, "w") after a universal-newline read, silently changing the on-disk encoding from CRLF to LF.
- 成因复核：实现与生成 SPEC 确有差异，但仓库现有证据不足以决定产品应采用哪种行为。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：filepath is the path of an existing, readable source file. keep_names is a collection of function identifiers; callers pass a non-empty set whose members are all extractable as functions from that file by the extraction backend. proj_dir is None or the path of the project directory containing the file; when not None, the file lies under proj_dir.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：When the file's extension identifies a supported source language and the file contains at least one detectable function, the file at filepath is edited in place so that the set of functions still extractable from it equals keep_names: every detected function whose identifier is not in keep_names is eliminated entirely, and every function whose identifier is in keep_names remains at its original location with unchanged content. Every line of the original file that does not belong to a removed function's definition (imports, module-level statements, comments, and other non-function text) is preserved, with retained lines keeping their original relative order. The returned pair of integers is (kept, removed): kept is the number of detected functions whose identifier is in keep_names and removed is the number of detected functions whose identifier is not, so kept + removed equals the total number of functions detected in the file. When every detected function is kept, the file is not rewritten and stays byte-identical. When the extension does not identify a supported language, or the file contains no detectable functions, the file is left unmodified and the returned pair is (0, 0). Whenever the file is rewritten, its on-disk encoding is the same as before the edit.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a tuple (kept, removed) of non-negative integers. Let S be the set of function spans detectable in the file (as returned by _function_spans for the file's language). Three cases arise: 1. UNSUPPORTED LANGUAGE: If the file extension (the substring after the last '.' in the basename, or '' if no '.') does not map to a language key in EXT_TO_LANG, the file on disk is unchanged and the return value is (0, 0). 2. NO DETECTED FUNCTIONS: If the extension maps to a valid language key but _function_spans returns an empty span list, the file on disk is unchanged and the return value is (0, 0). 3. FUNCTIONS DETECTED (spans is non-empty): Let kept = |{ (name, s, e) spans : name keep_names }| and removed = |{ (name, s, e) spans : name keep_names }|. The return value is (kept, removed). 3a. If removed == 0 (equivalently, drop is empty): the file on disk is unchanged. 3b. If removed > 0: the file at filepath is overwritten in place. Its new content consists of exactly those lines from the original file whose zero-based index i is NOT in the union of ranges [start, end] (inclusive) over all spans (name, start, end) where name keep_names. All non-function lines and all lines belonging to kept functions are preserved in their original order. The file is opened in 'w' mode and written via writelines. In all paths, keep_names, filepath, and proj_dir are not mutated. No except…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-040 — `src--env_check-py--_check_codegraph_version`
