The function never raises as a result of a missing or failing binary probe and performs no state modifications.

(Version not pinned) If config.settings.codegraph.version.strip().removeprefix('v') evaluates to the empty string, the function returns (True, None) immediately without invoking any subprocess. 2. (Codegraph not installed / unreachable) If the stripped-and-prefix-removed pinned version string is non-empty and either (a) executing [_codegraph_cmd(), '--version'] raises OSError or subprocess.SubprocessError, or (b) the captured stdout (stripped) is the empty string, the function returns (False, m) where m is a string of the form "codegraph (pinned v{want}) is not installed at {bin_dir} run ./install.sh (C/C++ extraction falls back to the regex extractor otherwise)." with want being the cleaned version and bin_dir being os.path.expanduser(config.settings.codegraph.bin_dir). 3. (Version mismatch) If the subprocess succeeds and its stripped stdout (got) is non-empty but got != want, the function returns (False, m) where m is a string of the form "codegraph {got} is installed but v{want} is pinned in fm-agent.toml re-run ./install.sh to install the pinned build." 4. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-041 — `src--env_check-py--_check_comment_checker`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/env_check-py/_check_comment_checker.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/env_check-py/_check_comment_checker.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--env_check-py--_check_comment_checker.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--env_check-py--_check_comment_checker.py)
- 触发/冲突：A non-object valid JSON value (e.g. null, [], 42) in the oh-my-openagent config file makes cfg.get(...) raise an uncaught AttributeError after json.load succeeds, violating the spec guarantee that the function never raises for non-object configuration content.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：None required of the caller. The oh-my-openagent configuration file at the well-known user-level OpenCode configuration path may be absent, unreadable, malformed, or semantically incomplete; this function must tolerate every such state without raising.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a tuple (ok, msg) where ok is a bool and msg is a str or None. ok is True and msg is None if and only if the configuration file exists at the well-known oh-my-openagent configuration path, parses as valid JSON, and its declared list of disabled hooks includes an entry disabling the comment-checker hook. Otherwise ok is False and msg is a non-empty human-readable string that identifies the configuration file's path and states which failure condition occurred: the file does not exist; the file could not be read or parsed as JSON; or the configuration parses but the comment-checker hook is not disabled. In the latter case msg additionally explains that FM-Agent writes function specifications as comment blocks which an enabled comment-checker hook may intercept, and states the configuration change required to disable the hook. The function never reads interactively, never modifies the configuration file, and never raises for absent, unreadable, malformed, or non-object configuration content.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function _check_comment_checker() terminates and returns a 2-tuple (success, message) where success is a bool and message is Optional[str]. Exactly one of the following mutually exclusive cases holds: 1. FILE_ABSENT: If os.path.exists(OH_MY_OPENAGENT_CONFIG) is False, then success == False and message == f"oh-my-openagent config not found at {OH_MY_OPENAGENT_CONFIG}". 2. READ_OR_PARSE_FAILURE: If the file exists but opening it raises IOError or json.load raises json.JSONDecodeError, then success == False and message == f"Failed to read {OH_MY_OPENAGENT_CONFIG}: {e}" where e is the caught exception. 3. HOOK_NOT_DISABLED: If the file is successfully read and parsed into a dict cfg, and "comment-checker" is NOT an element of cfg.get("disabled_hooks", []), then success == False and message is the multi-line string beginning with "comment-checker hook is NOT disabled..." instructing the user to add the disabled_hooks entry. 4. HOOK_DISABLED (success path): If the file is successfully read and parsed into a dict cfg, and "comment-checker" IS an element of cfg.get("disabled_hooks", []), then success == True and message is None. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-042 — `src--env_check-py--is_interactive`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/env_check-py/is_interactive.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/env_check-py/is_interactive.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--env_check-py--is_interactive.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--env_check-py--is_interactive.py)
- 触发/冲突：Setting sys.stdin to None (detached stdin) and calling is_interactive() raises AttributeError instead of returning False, violating the spec guarantee that the function never raises and returns False when stdin is not connected to a terminal.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：None. The process has a standard input stream binding, whether attached to a terminal, redirected, or detached; the function imposes no obligation on the caller.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True if and only if the process's standard input stream is connected to a terminal device, i.e., the current session is interactive and able to answer prompts issued in it. Returns False exactly when standard input is not a terminal — it is redirected from a file or pipe, or the session has no controlling terminal. The return value is a pure predicate of the terminal-attachment state of standard input at call time: the function has no side effects, performs no prompting itself, never raises, and repeated calls without a change in terminal attachment return the same value.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a boolean value. In the normal execution path: (1) It returns True if and only if sys.stdin is a valid, open stream object whose underlying file descriptor is associated with a terminal (tty) device, i.e., sys.stdin.isatty() evaluates to True. (2) It returns False if sys.stdin is a valid, open stream object not associated with a terminal (e.g., redirected from a file, pipe, or /dev/null). Formally: result {True, False} (result = True isatty(fileno(sys.stdin)) = True) (result = False isatty(fileno(sys.stdin)) = False). No state mutation occurs; sys.stdin and all global state remain unchanged. Exception paths: if sys.stdin is None (fully detached with no stream object), an AttributeError is raised; if sys.stdin refers to a closed file object, a ValueError ('I/O operation on closed file') is raised. In either exception case, no value is returned and the exception propagates to the caller. The function has no side effects and performs no I/O beyond the isatty() system call (fstat/isatty on the underlying file descriptor).

</details>

When the signature declares an operator overload — the keyword "operator" followed, ignoring whitespace, by an operator token consisting of a bracket pair, a run of operator punctuation characters, or a memory-management operator with optional array-form brackets — and that overload is immediately followed, ignoring whitespace, by an opening parenthesis, returns the overload name exactly as written in the signature, including the "operator" keyword, the intervening whitespace, and the operator token; this case takes precedence over generic identifier matching. Otherwise, returns the first identifier in signature_text, read left to right, that is immediately followed by an opening parenthesis (modulo whitespace) and is not one of the language's reserved keywords; identifiers occurring inside balanced angle-bracket segments (template or generic parameter lists) of the signature are excluded from this selection. Returns None when the signature contains neither an operator overload declaration nor such an identifier. The returned name is the raw declared identifier: no '/'-to-'_' canonicalization and no in-file duplicate-name suffixing are applied. The function does not modify signature_text or lang_cfg.

This path is taken regardless of lang_keywords. 2. **First non-keyword identifier before '(':** If path 1 does not match, _strip_angle_brackets(signature_text) is called producing a string `cleaned` in which all angle-bracket-enclosed segments are removed. The function then scans `cleaned` left-to-right for occurrences matching the pattern: word-boundary, one-or-more word-characters (captured as `name`), optional whitespace, '('. It returns the first captured…

The scan examines lines strictly in ascending order and, within each line, characters in ascending position order. Unmatched / not-found case: If no qualifying opening '{' is encountered in lines[start_idx:], or if a qualifying opening '{' is found but its matching '}' does not appear before the end of lines, then r == len(lines) - 1. 4. Test files, missing files, and files with unmapped extensions contribute neither to the written nor the skipped count. If no function is written and none is skipped, the function still returns (0, 0) without raising. FileNotFoundError is raised if and only if the phases.json file does not exist at the resolved location. The function never modifies any source file under proj_dir. If the file at os.path.join(work_dir or proj_dir, 'phases.json') does not exist, a FileNotFoundError is raised with a message containing 'phases.json not found at'. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-049 — `src--file_utils-py--_is_under_submodules`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/file_utils-py/_is_under_submodules.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/file_utils-py/_is_under_submodules.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--file_utils-py--_is_under_submodules.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--file_utils-py--_is_under_submodules.py)
- 触发/冲突：Passing an empty collection as an iterator or generator (e.g. iter([]) or an empty generator) makes the truthiness guard `if not submodules:` fail — iterators are always truthy in Python — so _is_under_submodules falls into the any(...) check, which is vacuously False, returning False instead of the True the specification requires for any empty collection.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：rel_path is a project-relative file path string using '/' as the separator. submodules is either None (meaning no restriction) or an iterable of project-relative directory path strings using '/' as the separator.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True when submodules is None or an empty collection, meaning no submodule restriction applies and every path passes. When submodules is non-empty, returns True if and only if rel_path designates a file located under (in or below) at least one of the given submodule directories. Containment is decided at directory boundaries: rel_path passes for a submodule exactly when rel_path equals that submodule's path or begins with that submodule's path followed by a single '/' separator, so a path under a different directory that merely shares the submodule path as a string prefix does not pass. Returns False when rel_path is not under any of the given submodule directories. The decision depends only on the input path strings: the function performs no I/O, mutates no state, and never raises for any combination of input values.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a boolean value `result`. No side effects occur and no mutable state is modified. Formally: (1) If `submodules` is falsy (i.e., `submodules is None` or `submodules` is an empty iterable), then `result == True`. (2) If `submodules` is truthy, define `norm` as the string obtained from `rel_path` by (a) replacing every occurrence of the backslash character '\' with '/', and (b) iteratively removing the prefix './' from the beginning until the string no longer starts with './'. Then `result == True` if and only if there exists an element `sub` in `submodules` such that (`norm == sub`) OR (`norm` starts with the string `sub + '/'`). Otherwise `result == False`. (3) Under the given pre-condition (rel_path is a str, submodules is None or an iterable of str), no exception is raised. In logical notation: result = (not submodules) OR ( sub submodules : norm == sub norm.startswith(sub + '/')), where norm = strip_leading_dot_slash(rel_path.replace('\\', '/')).

</details>

- 触发/冲突：Pointing _json_file_is_valid at an existing, readable file whose bytes are not decodable in the default text encoding (e.g. a leading 0xFF byte) makes json.load's text-mode read raise UnicodeDecodeError — a ValueError subclass not covered by the except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) clause — which propagates to the caller instead of the function returning False as the specification requires for any JSON-parse failure.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True if and only if a file exists at path, is openable for reading, and its entire content parses as a single valid JSON document. Returns False when the path does not exist, does not designate a readable regular file, or the file content cannot be parsed as JSON, whatever the cause of the failure. Does not raise for any of these failure conditions, and does not write to or otherwise modify the file at path.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a boolean value `result`. Formally: (result == True) (the file at `path` exists, is accessible for reading, and its entire content is successfully parsed as valid JSON by `json.load`). (result == False) (an OSError was raised during `open(path, 'r')` or during readingcovering FileNotFoundError, PermissionError, IsADirectoryError, and other I/O failuresor a json.JSONDecodeError was raised because the file content is not valid JSON). If any exception not in {OSError, json.JSONDecodeError} is raised (e.g., UnicodeDecodeError from malformed encoding, MemoryError, RecursionError from deeply nested JSON), it propagates uncaught to the caller. In all paths where the file is successfully opened, the `with` statement guarantees the file descriptor is closed before the function returns or the exception propagates. The file is opened in read-only mode ('r'), so no mutation of the file or filesystem occurs. The input `path` is not modified. No global state is altered. path: result {True, False} an unhandled exception is raised.

</details>

Otherwise it returns False. Formally: (name = "" result = False) (name "" "::" name result = (name first_line)) (name "" "::" name result = ( i,j. first_line[i:j] matches the pattern (?<![A-Za-z0-9_])name(?:\s*\(|\b))) The input strings first_line and name are not mutated. <details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：layers_spec is the run's layer-range specification, written in one of exactly two forms: a single non-negative decimal integer 'N' denoting exactly layer N, or 'A-B' with non-negative decimal integers A <= B denoting every layer in the inclusive interval [A, B]. Whitespace may surround the input and may appear adjacent to the range separator; it carries no meaning.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a pair of integers (start, end) with start <= end such that the inclusive interval [start, end] contains exactly the layer indices named by layers_spec: a single-integer specification returns that integer as both start and end (start == end), and a range specification returns the left operand as start and the right operand as end, preserving the order given. Surrounding whitespace does not alter the returned values. Error contract: raises ValueError when a range specification's start exceeds its end, and raises ValueError when the input conforms to neither documented form (including any token that is not a decimal integer, extra separators, or empty operands; negative layer indices are unrepresentable because the separator reserves the '-' character), instead of returning.

Under the given pre-condition the ValueError branch (start > end) is unreachable, so no exception path exists. The returned pair (lo, hi) faithfully encodes the layer range described by layers_spec: a single-layer spec 'N' yields (N, N), and a range spec 'A-B' yields (A, B) with A B.

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-059 — `src--generate_topdown_layers-py--_get_call_regex`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/generate_topdown_layers-py/_get_call_regex.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/generate_topdown_layers-py/_get_call_regex.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--generate_topdown_layers-py--_get_call_regex.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--generate_topdown_layers-py--_get_call_regex.py)
- 触发/冲突：Including "c" in the template-syntax branch makes _get_call_regex("c") admit a non-whitespace '<[^>]*>' segment between identifier and '(', so the pure C comparison expression 'a < b > (c)' is falsely detected as a call site of 'a'.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：lang_key is a language key recognized by the run's language configuration, i.e., a language for which regex-fallback call-site detection is performed.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the call-site detection pattern for lang_key: a compiled regular expression such that, in any source text, each non-overlapping match corresponds to one call-expression occurrence in lang_key's syntax and captures in group 1 exactly the identifier forming the callee name of that call, followed in the match by the opening parenthesis that begins the call's argument list. The captured name is always a whole identifier: it is anchored at an identifier boundary, so it can never be the suffix of a longer identifier. Between the identifier and the opening parenthesis the pattern admits arbitrary whitespace and, only for languages whose syntax admits generic/template/type-argument notation on a call site, an optional bracketed type-argument sequence of that notation; for languages without such notation, no non-whitespace text is admitted in that gap. The returned pattern depends only on lang_key: calls with the same lang_key return patterns with identical matching behavior, regardless of prior calls. The call performs no file I/O, mutates no global or configuration state, and raises no exception for any recognized lang_key.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates normally and returns a compiled regular expression object (an instance of re.Pattern). No exceptions are raised. The returned regex object contains exactly one capturing group (group 1) designed to capture a function/method identifier at a call site. The specific pattern is determined by lang_key as follows: (1) If lang_key {"cpp", "c", "java", "typescript", "javascript", "cuda", "arkts"}, the returned pattern is re.compile(r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:<[^>]*>)?\s*\("), matching an identifier followed by optional C++/Java-style template arguments and an opening parenthesis. (2) If lang_key == "rust", the returned pattern is re.compile(r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:::<[^>]*>)?\s*\("), matching an identifier followed by an optional Rust turbofish (::<...>) and an opening parenthesis. (3) If lang_key == "go", the returned pattern is re.compile(r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:\[[^\]]*\])?\s*\("), matching an identifier followed by optional Go-style type parameters ([...]) and an opening parenthesis. (4) Otherwise (e.g., Python, Ruby, Shell, SQL), the returned pattern is re.compile(r"\b(\w+)\s*\("), matching an identifier followed directly by an opening parenthesis. Formally: lang_key satisfying the pre-condition, p : re.Pattern such that _get_call_regex(lang_key) = p p.groups == 1 p.pattern {r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:<[^>]*>)?\s*\(", r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:::<[^>]*>)?\s*\(", r"\b(\w+)\s*(?:\[[^\]]*\])?\s*\(", r"\b(\w+)\s…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-060 — `src--git-py--_get_head_commit`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/git-py/_get_head_commit.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/git-py/_get_head_commit.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--git-py--_get_head_commit.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--git-py--_get_head_commit.py)
- 触发/冲突：With the git executable absent from PATH, subprocess.run raises FileNotFoundError (an OSError), which _get_head_commit does not catch — it catches only subprocess.CalledProcessError — so the exception propagates to the caller instead of the spec-required None return.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：proj_dir is an existing directory path on the local filesystem, and a git executable is available to the process.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：If proj_dir lies inside a git repository whose HEAD resolves to an existing commit, returns the full commit id (40-character hex object name) of the commit that HEAD refers to in proj_dir, as a string with no leading or trailing whitespace. Otherwise (proj_dir is not inside any git repository, or the repository has no commits yet), returns None. The function never raises for a non-repository or commit-less directory; that situation is only observable via the None return value. Any non-None return value is suitable to be recorded as a processed-commit baseline (appended to fm_agent/version.log) and later used as a diff base for incremental comparison.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a value `result`. Exactly one of the following holds: 1. **Normal (git-success) path:** `proj_dir` is inside a valid Git repository with at least one commit. `result` is a `str` equal to the stripped standard-output of `git -C proj_dir rev-parse HEAD`, i.e., the full hexadecimal commit hash of the current HEAD (e.g., a 40-char SHA-1 or 64-char SHA-256 string with no leading/trailing whitespace). No exception is raised. 2. **Git-failure path (CalledProcessError):** `proj_dir` is not a Git repository, or the repository has no commits (HEAD is unborn). `subprocess.run` raises `subprocess.CalledProcessError` (because `check=True` and the exit code is non-zero). The exception is caught; a `logging.info` call is emitted with the message `"_get_head_commit: %s is not a git repo."` formatted with `proj_dir`. `result` is `None`. No exception propagates to the caller. 3. **Unhandled-exception path:** An exception other than `subprocess.CalledProcessError` is raised (e.g., `OSError`, `FileNotFoundError` if the pre-condition on git availability is violated, or a filesystem error). The exception propagates to the caller uncaught; no value is returned. Formal logic (for paths 1 and 2, assuming the pre-condition holds): (result str result == strip(stdout(git_rev_parse_HEAD(proj_dir))) git_exit_code(proj_dir) == 0) (result is None git_exit_code(proj_dir) 0 log_emitted("_g…

</details>

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/git-py/frozen_worktree.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/git-py/frozen_worktree.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--git-py--frozen_worktree.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--git-py--frozen_worktree.py)
- 触发/冲突：In the non-git fallback path, shutil.ignore_patterns(*exclude) matches exclude names against basenames at every depth of the tree, so a nested src/fm_agent/ directory is dropped from the snapshot even though only the top-level fm_agent directory may be excluded.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：proj_dir is the path of an existing directory whose current content the pipeline must analyze without further modification. exclude is a tuple of directory names, relative to proj_dir, that must be kept out of the snapshot's git commit. copy_excluded is a boolean stating whether the caller still needs the excluded directories' prior-run artifacts to be physically readable from disk inside the snapshot (incremental/resume runs pass true; full fresh runs pass false).
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：The function is used as a context manager and yields exactly one value: the path of an isolated "snapshot" directory located inside a freshly created temporary directory whose name embeds the basename of proj_dir, so concurrent snapshots of different projects are distinguishable and never share a location. The yielded directory is a faithful capture of proj_dir as of call time: it contains all of proj_dir's committed content plus all uncommitted modifications and untracked files, so the pipeline can run against it while later concurrent edits to proj_dir have no effect on the snapshot. When proj_dir is a git repository with at least one commit, proj_dir's real git index and working tree are never modified by building the snapshot, and none of the directory names in exclude appears in the snapshot's commit tree regardless of copy_excluded. When copy_excluded is true, every name in exclude that exists as a directory under proj_dir additionally exists inside the snapshot as a copy that preserves symlinks, making prior-run workspace artifacts available from disk; when copy_excluded is false, the excluded directories are entirely absent from the snapshot. When proj_dir is not a git repository with a commit, the snapshot is instead a full copy of the directory tree that preserves symlinks and likewise does not materialize the excluded directories. Before yielding, the function annou…

SNAPSHOT EXISTS: A directory `wt` exists at path `{base}/snapshot` where `base` is a freshly created temporary directory named `fm_agent_wt_{repo_name}_*`. The generator yields the absolute path string `wt`. 2. PROJ_DIR UNMODIFIED: The directory `proj_dir` (resolved to its absolute form) and its contents, including its git index and working tree, are unchanged. All git staging operations used a private GIT_INDEX_FILE located in `base`. 3. FAITHFUL SNAPSHOT: `wt` contains a complete snapshot of `proj_dir`'s state at function entry, including committed content, uncommitted tracked modifications, and untracked files. 4. GIT PATH (is_git=True, i.e., `git rev-parse --verify HEAD` succeeded): a. A new commit object (message 'fm_agent snapshot', parent HEAD) exists in proj_dir's git object store. b. A detached worktree is registered at `wt` pointing to that commit. c. The directories named in `exclude` are NOT part of the committed tree (removed via `git rm --cached` from the private index before write-tree). d. Cleanup requires `git -C {proj_dir} worktree remove --force {wt}`. 5. NON-GIT PATH (is_git=False, i.e., rev-parse raised CalledProcessError): a. `wt` is a plain recursive copy of `proj_dir` (symlinks preserved), created via shutil.copytree. b. Entries…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-062 — `src--incremental_reasoner-py--_bare_function_name`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/incremental_reasoner-py/_bare_function_name.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/incremental_reasoner-py/_bare_function_name.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--incremental_reasoner-py--_bare_function_name.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--incremental_reasoner-py--_bare_function_name.py)
- 触发/冲突：Any identifier whose unqualified function name legitimately ends with underscore-followed-by-digits (e.g. "MyModule.foo_1") has that suffix unconditionally stripped, mangling foo_1 into foo and making it indistinguishable from a distinct function named foo.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：name is a canonical extraction identifier of a function, possibly class-qualified whenever the language's extraction distinguishes same-named members of different classes, originating from either the file-local recognizer or the whole-project index. The identifier is a non-empty string whose unqualified tail is the function's own name.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the unqualified function name: the identifier stripped of any class or namespace qualification, so that identifiers naming the same function in different classes reduce to the same bare name, while names of distinct functions remain distinct. Any numeric disambiguation suffix that an extractor may append to distinguish repeated same-named definitions within one file is not part of the function name and does not appear in the result. The result is deterministic for a given input and identical regardless of which extractor produced the input identifier. The returned value is a string; the input is not mutated and no exception is raised for any well-formed extraction identifier.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` such that: (1) Let `parts` be the list produced by `re.split(r"::|\.", identifier)`; then `tail = parts[-1]` is the unqualified (last) segment of `identifier` after splitting on `::` or `.`. (2) `result` equals `re.sub(r"_\d+$", "", tail)`, i.e., `tail` with any trailing deduplication suffix of the form underscore-followed-by-one-or-more-digits removed. (3) Under the given pre-condition (identifier is a non-empty canonical extraction identifier whose unqualified tail is the function's own name), `result` is a non-empty string representing the bare function name without any class/module qualification and without any extractor-appended numeric dedup suffix. (4) No exception is raised: `identifier` is guaranteed to be a non-empty string, so `re.split` returns a list with at least one element, making `[-1]` safe, and `re.sub` operates on a valid string. Formally: identifier satisfying the pre-condition, ! result str such that result = re.sub(r"_\d+$", "", re.split(r"::|\.", identifier)[-1]) len(result) 1 result contains no `::` or `.` separators result does not end with a substring matching r"_\d+$".

</details>

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：proj_dir is an existing git repository whose working tree may contain modifications and untracked files relative to old_commit_id. old_commit_id identifies an existing commit in that repository, typically the commit recorded by a previous full pipeline run as the baseline for incremental analysis. submodules is either None, meaning the whole repository is in scope, or a list of directory paths inside proj_dir delimiting the analyzed scope.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a mapping whose keys are the absolute paths of source files and whose value for each key is a change record exposing exactly three name lists, 'added', 'removed', and 'modified', each a sorted list of pairwise-distinct function names. Membership invariants per listed file: 'added' contains exactly the functions present in the current working-tree version of the file and absent from its version at old_commit_id; 'removed' contains exactly the functions present at old_commit_id and absent from the current version; 'modified' contains exactly the functions present in both revisions whose source text differs between them. The three lists of one file are pairwise disjoint, so each function of the file that changed at the function level is classified in exactly one list, and a function appearing in one list of a file appears in no other list of that file. File-inclusion invariants: a file has an entry only if its extension corresponds to a recognized source language, its content or existence differs between old_commit_id and the working tree (a file present in the working tree but absent from old_commit_id, including an untracked one, counting as new), it is not a test file per the project's test-file naming convention, it lies outside the pipeline's own working-area directory tree, and it falls inside the submodule scope when one is given. Files whose differences are confin…
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Upon normal completion (line 142 `return result`), the following state holds: 1. RETURN VALUE: The function returns `result`, a dict mapping absolute file paths (str) to change-description dicts. Specifically, result {os.path.abspath(os.path.join(proj_dir, rp)) : rp files '.' rp rp.rsplit('.',1)[-1] EXT_TO_LANG}. Only files with at least one added, removed, or modified function appear as keys. 2. PER-FILE ENTRY STRUCTURE: For each abs_path in result, result[abs_path] is a dict with exactly three keys: 'added', 'removed', 'modified'. Each value is a sorted list of function-name strings. If `bare == stem` (a free function with no class qualifier), the file is registered under the single key `stem` only. **Completeness and exclusivity:** The index contains exactly the entries described above and no others. (3) The return value is os.path.join(work_dir, f"incremental_{timestamp}.log"), the path to the newly designated log file for this run. (4) The root logger (logging.getLogger()) has its level set to logging.INFO and its handler list replaced with exactly two handlers in this order: (a) a logging.FileHandler whose stream is the log file at the returned path, and (b) a logging.StreamHandler whose stream is the real underlying console stream (i.e., the stream that was sys.stdout before any _StdoutTee wrapping, recovered via getattr(sys.stdout, '_console', sys.stdout)). Both handlers carry the formatter "%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s". All handlers previously attached to the root logger have been removed. (5) sys.stdout is now an _StdoutTee instance constructed with (console_stream, file_handler.stream), where console_stream is the real console stream identified in step (4b). The caller treats this identically to the unsupported-language case and falls back to the regex extractor. **Normal return list of spans:** If the query returns one or more rows, the function closes the database connection and returns a list of 3-tuples `[(ident_i, start_i, end_i), ...]` where: - Each `ident_i` equals `_extraction_ident(name_i, qualified_name_i)`, producing the canonical, filesystem-safe, scope-qualified identifier (parts joined by '::', '/' replaced by '_'). - `start_i = int(codegraph_start_line_i) - 1` and `end_i = int(codegraph_end_line_i) - 1`, converting from codegraph's 1-indexed inclusive convention to the 0-indexed inclusive convention expected by callers. …

(1) Scope reduction: no scope separator and no scope prefix occurs in the result; however deeply the component is qualified, the result identifies only the innermost declared entity. (3) Operator overloads: when the innermost component declares an operator overload — the keyword 'operator' followed, with any intervening whitespace ignored for recognition, by an operator token — the result is that overload's name formed from the keyword 'operator' together with the operator token, where the token is a bracket pair, a memory-management operator with or without array-form brackets (whitespace variants inside such a token yield the same name), or the maximal leading run of operator punctuation characters. (4) Idempotence on bare input: when name is already an undecorated identifier, the result is that identifier itself, with surrounding whitespace (if any) removed. (5) Emptiness contract: the result is empty if and only if name contains no non-whitespace characters; every component containing a rec…

Otherwise, let `tail` be defined as: if `"::" in s`, then `tail = s.rsplit("::", 1)[1].lstrip()`; elif `"." in s`, then `tail = s.rsplit(".", 1)[1].lstrip()`; else `tail = s`. 3. Elif `re.fullmatch(r'delete(?:\s*\[\s*\])?', rest)`, then `result == "operator delete[]"` if `"[" in rest` else `"operator delete"`. f. Else, collect the maximal prefix of `rest` consisting solely of characters in `"+-*/%&|^~!=<>,"` into `symbol`. If `symbol` is non-empty, `result == "operator" + "".join(symbol)`. g. If none of (b)(f) matched, fall through to step 4. 4. If `re.search(r'(?:^|::|\.)(\w+)$', s)` matches, `result` is the captured group (the trailing word-component of `s`). 5. #### QWEN-MISMATCH-080 — `src--languages--erlang-py--ElpClient::_wait_for_response`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/languages/erlang-py/ElpClient::_wait_for_response.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/languages/erlang-py/ElpClient::_wait_for_response.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--languages--erlang-py--ElpClient::_wait_for_response.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--languages--erlang-py--ElpClient::_wait_for_response.py)
- 触发/冲突：ElpClient.request receives a JSON-RPC error response whose 'error' value is falsy (e.g. {"id": 1, "error": {}}); the truthiness guard `if error:` skips both exception branches and returns None, silently treating a server error as a successful result-less reply instead of raising an exception.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：The caller has already delivered to the server the request bearing identifier request_id, and that request's response is still pending — the call is entered only in the interval between request delivery and response receipt. deadline is an absolute monotonic-clock time bound that covers the entire enclosing request call, including any retries the caller may perform on transient conflicts; this call must respect that existing bound and never wait past it.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Blocks until either the server's response to the request identified by request_id arrives or the deadline passes, whichever occurs first. A message qualifies as the awaited response only if it carries the identifier request_id and is a response rather than a server-initiated request or notification. When the matching response arrives and reports success, the call returns the decoded result payload carried by that response — exactly what the server reported as the result of that request — and returns None when the response carries no result; a None or otherwise falsy return never signals failure, only the absence of server-reported data. Server-to-client traffic carrying other identities that arrives while waiting is consumed, and answered where the protocol requires an answer, so such traffic can never block, indefinitely delay, or be mistaken for the awaited response. Error contract: an exception is raised when the deadline passes before the matching response arrives; when the matching response reports a server error for the request — where a content-modification conflict (the server observed the document content changed while serving the request) is raised as a distinct exception class that identifies exactly that condition, so the caller can retry only that class, while every other server error is raised under a different exception class; and when the transport fails, the m…
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The method terminates (it cannot loop indefinitely) because every iteration calls _next_message(deadline), which enforces the absolute monotonic-clock bound and raises on expiry. Exactly one of the following outcomes holds upon exit: 1. NORMAL RETURN: A transport message m was received such that m.get('id') == request_id, 'method' is not a key of m, and m.get('error') is falsy (None or absent). The method returns m.get('result'), which is the decoded result payload of the awaited response. The response message is fully consumed and not re-presented. 2. CONTENT-MODIFIED ERROR: A matching response m (same id, no 'method' key) was received whose m.get('error') is a dict e with e.get('code') == _CONTENT_MODIFIED_ERROR. The method raises _ContentModifiedError(e). No value is returned. 3. GENERIC SERVER ERROR: A matching response m was received whose m.get('error') is truthy but does not satisfy the content-modified predicate above. The method raises RuntimeError with message f'ELP request failed: {error}'. No value is returned. 4. TIMEOUT / TRANSPORT FAILURE: Before any matching response arrived, _next_message raised an exception (deadline elapsed, transport error, reader failure, or server-process termination). That exception propagates unmodified out of _wait_for_response. No value is returned. In all cases: Every message received by _next_message prior to the terminating message…

</details>

(3) .spans maps the absolute path of each analyzed source file with detected functions to a non-empty list of (function identifier, start line, end line) triples using 0-based, inclusive line numbers, covering every function the analysis attributes to that file; files with no detected functions have no entry at all in .…

Case 2 (analysis failure): If _analyze_project(proj_dir) raised any Exception exc, then (a) a warning is emitted via logging.warning with the message 'ELP Erlang analysis unavailable for <proj_dir>: <exc>', and (b) the returned value is a freshly constructed ErlangAnalysis whose .functions is the empty dict {}, whose .edges is the empty dict {}, and whose .spans is an empty mapping containing no entry for any file. No partially populated analysis record is returned. In both cases the returned object is a plain data container; no I/O or project mutation is performed by the cons…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-084 — `src--languages--erlang-py--_function_id`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/languages/erlang-py/_function_id.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/languages/erlang-py/_function_id.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--languages--erlang-py--_function_id.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--languages--erlang-py--_function_id.py)
- 触发/冲突：A label whose module part is a quoted atom (e.g. 'MyModule':myfunc/2) satisfies the name.startswith("'") guard, so the module qualification embedded in the label is incorrectly kept in the name component instead of being discarded in favor of the URI-derived module.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：uri is the document URI of an Erlang source document — the defining document reported for the symbol by the analysis backend, or the analyzed document when the symbol carries none — and the document's Erlang module is derivable from that URI. label is the function symbol's reported name: an Erlang function designator in name/arity form, optionally carrying a module-qualification prefix.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a single string that is the module-qualified Erlang function identifier of the symbol, per Erlang identity conventions: it combines the defining module derived from uri, the function name, and the integer arity parsed from label. The identifier's module component always comes from the document URI; any module qualification embedded in label is discarded in favor of the URI-derived module, except that a label name written as a quoted atom is preserved in full as the name component. The identifier is filesystem-safe: each component is sanitized so the result contains no character that would corrupt path names or identifier structure, and every character outside the ASCII alphanumeric-and-underscore set is replaced by a deterministic escape derived solely from that character. The function is deterministic: identical (uri, label) pairs always yield the identical identifier, and no backend round-trip is performed. The arity portion of the result is exactly the integer arity text parsed from label. Raises ValueError — and no other exception type — precisely when no well-formed identifier can be derived: label is not of the form name/arity with an integer arity, or the module cannot be derived from uri. Never returns None.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function _function_id(uri, label) terminates in one of the following states: 1. RAISES ValueError (invalid arity): If label does not contain '/' (causing rsplit to fail) or the substring after the last '/' is not a valid base-10 integer literal (causing int() to fail), a ValueError is raised with the message f"ELP function label has no valid arity: {label!r}", chained (via 'from') from the original ValueError or TypeError. 2. RAISES ValueError (unresolvable URI): If _module_from_uri(uri) raises ValueError because uri cannot be resolved to a well-formed Erlang module name, that ValueError propagates unmodified. 3. NORMAL RETURN: Otherwise, the function returns a string of the form "{E}__{F}__{A}" where: - Let (raw_name, A) = label.rsplit('/', 1), so A is the arity substring (guaranteed to be a valid integer literal). - Let effective_name = raw_name.rsplit(':', 1)[1] if (':' in raw_name AND NOT raw_name.startswith("'")) else raw_name. That is, a module-qualification prefix (everything up to and including the last ':') is stripped from raw_name only when raw_name contains ':' and does not begin with a single-quote character (which would indicate a quoted Erlang atom where ':' is part of the name). - Let M = _module_from_uri(uri), the Erlang module name derived from the document URI. - E = _escape_component(M): a filesystem-safe ASCII-alphanumeric-and-underscore rendering of M…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-085 — `src--languages--erlang-py--_iter_project_files`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/languages/erlang-py/_iter_project_files.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/languages/erlang-py/_iter_project_files.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--languages--erlang-py--_iter_project_files.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--languages--erlang-py--_iter_project_files.py)
- 触发/冲突：Calling the public REGISTRY['erlang'].batch_extract with an untraversable (nonexistent) proj_dir returns a successful empty extraction {} because _iter_project_files' os.walk silently swallows the traversal OSError (default onerror=None), whereas the spec requires OSError, which would surface through the public API as None.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：proj_dir is the path of an existing directory to scan — callers pass the normalized absolute path of the project root. suffixes is a non-empty set of lowercase dot-prefixed file extensions selecting which files qualify.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Lazily yields the absolute path of every file located anywhere under proj_dir whose filename extension matches one of the given suffixes case-insensitively (the file's extension is case-folded before being compared against suffixes), and yields no other paths. The traversal descends into every subdirectory of proj_dir except subdirectories whose names match, case-insensitively, a fixed set of directory names that project analysis conventionally excludes. Excluded directories are pruned wholesale: no file located directly in an excluded directory or anywhere within its subtree is ever yielded. The exclusion covers pipeline work directories, so files copied into nested pipeline work directories never appear among the yielded paths. Every qualifying file is yielded exactly once; every yielded path is absolute and lies under proj_dir. No ordering of yielded paths is guaranteed — callers requiring a deterministic total order impose it themselves. The function is lazy: paths are produced only on demand from the iterator. The directory tree is not modified, and no cache or other observable state is consulted or updated. Raises OSError when the directory tree under proj_dir cannot be traversed.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a generator object (no body code executes until the first next() call). When the generator is fully consumed, it yields a sequence of strings representing absolute file paths. Formally, the set of yielded values Y satisfies: p Y: (1) p = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(root, filename)) for some (root, filename) encountered during os.walk(proj_dir); (2) Path(filename).suffix.lower() suffixes; (3) no ancestor directory d of p relative to proj_dir has d.lower() _SKIP_DIRS (pruned via in-place mutation dirs[:] = [...]). Conversely, every file reachable from proj_dir via os.walk whose lowercased suffix is in suffixes and whose ancestor directories are not in _SKIP_DIRS (case-insensitive) is yielded exactly once. Yield order follows os.walk top-down traversal order. If proj_dir is unreadable or os.walk encounters OS errors, they are silently ignored (default onerror=None), potentially yielding fewer or zero results. No exception is raised by the generator body itself under normal filesystem conditions. The input arguments proj_dir and suffixes are not mutated. The local dirs list is mutated in-place each iteration to prune skipped directories, but this does not affect the caller's state. If no files match, the generator yields nothing and terminates normally (StopIteration).

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-086 — `src--languages--erlang-py--_module_from_uri`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/languages/erlang-py/_module_from_uri.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/languages/erlang-py/_module_from_uri.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--languages--erlang-py--_module_from_uri.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--languages--erlang-py--_module_from_uri.py)
- 触发/冲突：For a URI whose document path yields no base name (e.g. uri=""), the function returns an empty string instead of raising ValueError as the specification requires.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：uri is the document URI of an Erlang source document — the defining document reported for a function symbol by the analysis backend, or the analyzed document itself when the symbol carries none. The URI identifies a concrete document file, and that file's base name (its last path segment minus its file extension) is the Erlang module of the document, so a module name is derivable from the URI.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the Erlang module name derived from uri: the last path segment of the URI's document path with exactly its file extension removed, so the returned string contains no path-separator character and no file extension. Both forward and backward slashes in the document path are honored as path separators when locating the last segment, and every percent-encoded sequence present in the URI is decoded to its corresponding character before derivation, so the returned name contains no percent-encoded escapes. The function is pure and deterministic: identical uri inputs always yield the identical string, and derivation performs no I/O and no analysis-backend round-trip. The returned name is the file base name verbatim apart from extension stripping — no sanitization, case folding, or other transformation is applied, so the result may still contain characters that a caller must sanitize before embedding it in a filesystem-safe identifier. Raises ValueError precisely when uri cannot be resolved to a well-formed module name — that is, when the document path yields no base name usable as a module name. Never returns None.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `m` such that: (1) `m` is of type `str`; (2) Let `raw_path = urlparse(uri).path`, `decoded_path = unquote(raw_path)`, and `norm_path = decoded_path.replace('\\', '/')`. Then `m == PurePosixPath(norm_path).stem`, i.e., `m` is the final component of the POSIX-normalized, percent-decoded path portion of `uri`, with its file extension (the last dot-separated suffix) removed. (3) By the pre-condition that `uri` identifies a concrete Erlang document file whose base name (last path segment minus extension) is the Erlang module, `m` is exactly the Erlang module name associated with the document. (4) If the decoded, normalized path is empty or has no file component, `m` is the empty string `''`. (5) No exception is raised under the stated pre-condition (the URI is well-formed and identifies a concrete file); however, if `uri` were malformed such that `urlparse` or `unquote` encountered an error, the corresponding exception (`ValueError`, etc.) would propagate. Formally: uri satisfying the pre-condition, ! m str m = PurePosixPath(unquote(urlparse(uri).path).replace('\\','/')).stem m = module_name(uri).

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-088 — `src--llm_client-py--_matches_inject_target`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/llm_client-py/_matches_inject_target.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/llm_client-py/_matches_inject_target.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--llm_client-py--_matches_inject_target.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--llm_client-py--_matches_inject_target.py)
- 触发/冲突：A full-URL inject target with a non-http(s) scheme (ftp://files.example.com/data.txt) that exactly prefixes the url is not classified as full-URL form on line 2 and falls through to the host-matching branch, so _matches_inject_target returns False where the spec requires True.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：url is a base URL string normalized so that an absent value becomes the empty string and trailing path-separator characters are stripped; target is a single non-empty configured provider-injection target string.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True if and only if url matches target under the module's two-form matching rule. A target expressed as a full URL (one that names a scheme plus URL prefix) matches if and only if url begins with that target string exactly. A target naming a host alone matches if and only if url's host portion equals the target, or is a subdomain of the target at a dot boundary — i.e., the target appears as a complete trailing host-label sequence of the host, so that hosts merely sharing a string suffix with the target without a separating dot do not match. Returns False in every other case, including when url's host portion cannot be determined (e.g., url is empty or malformed so no host can be extracted). The classification of a target as full-URL form versus host form does not depend on the case of the target. The function is a pure function of its arguments: it never mutates url or target, performs no observable side effect, and never raises for any combination of url and target values.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a boolean value and raises no exceptions. Let R denote the return value. There are three mutually exclusive execution paths: 1. If target.lower() starts with 'http://' or 'https://': R url.startswith(target) (case-sensitive prefix check of url against the original-case target string). 2. Else, if urllib.parse.urlparse(url) raises any Exception: R False. 3. Else (parsing succeeds): let host (urllib.parse.urlparse(url).hostname or ''), where hostname is lowercased per Python's urlparse semantics and None is coerced to ''. Then R (host == target) host.endswith('.' + target). Formally: (target.lower().startswith(('http://','https://')) R = url.startswith(target)) (target.lower().startswith(('http://','https://')) parse_raises(url) R = False) (target.lower().startswith(('http://','https://')) parse_raises(url) R = ((host = target) host.endswith('.' + target))) where host = (urlparse(url).hostname or '') No mutable state is modified; no exceptions escape the function. The inputs url and target are unchanged. Given the pre-condition that target is non-empty, the empty-string url (normalized absent value) will always yield R = False in paths 2 and 3, since '' target and ''.endswith('.'+target) is False.

</details>

- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：work_dir is the FM-Agent run workspace, i.e. the directory that is the root of this run's trace subtree. Tracing for the workspace may be enabled or disabled.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the single deterministic location of the trace-subtree root under work_dir — the region in which every trace artifact of this run lives: the structured event store, the captured payload files, and the raw LLM-traffic files. The result depends only on work_dir: identical inputs always yield the identical value, and distinct work_dir values map to distinct trace roots. The returned region is a direct subregion of work_dir and is suitable for path joining, so every per-event artifact path derived beneath it is unambiguously attributable to work_dir. The function performs no filesystem I/O: it creates no directory and no file and does not test existence; bringing any part of the trace region into existence is the obligation of whichever component later writes an artifact there. The returned value is the canonical trace-dir argument of the structured trace writers: it is falsy exactly when tracing is disabled for work_dir — in which case downstream writers record nothing — and truthy when tracing is enabled, and the function never raises solely because tracing is disabled.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string equal to os.path.join(work_dir, "trace"), representing the path to the 'trace' subdirectory under the FM-Agent run workspace directory. No filesystem side effects occur (the directory is not created, checked for existence, or modified). No global or local mutable state is altered. The pre-condition on work_dir (being the root of the run's trace subtree, with tracing possibly enabled or disabled) is unchanged and irrelevant to the computation, as the function purely constructs a path string. Formally: work_dir: str, result = _trace_dir(work_dir) result == os.path.join(work_dir, "trace") no filesystem mutation no exception raised (assuming work_dir is a valid string). If work_dir is not a string or path-like object, a TypeError is raised by os.path.join.

</details>

The `event_id` has the form `'opencode_<uuid4 hex>'` and is unique. **Outcome 2 CalledProcessError propagated:** If `_wait_opencode_process` returns a non-zero `exit_code` or a non-None `error`, or if `exit_code != 0` after the wait, a `subprocess.CalledProcessError` is raised (with `.returncode` equal to the subprocess exit code and `.cmd` equal to `command_argv(command)`). #### QWEN-MISMATCH-094 — `src--parser-py--_load_sidecar_json`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/parser-py/_load_sidecar_json.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/parser-py/_load_sidecar_json.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--parser-py--_load_sidecar_json.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--parser-py--_load_sidecar_json.py)
- 触发/冲突：When the sidecar file exists but contains invalid UTF-8 bytes, json.load on the UTF-8-opened file raises UnicodeDecodeError, which is neither OSError nor json.JSONDecodeError, so it escapes the except clause and propagates to the caller instead of mapping to a None return.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：file_path is the path of the extracted function file being processed, and suffix is the filename suffix appended directly to file_path to form the sidecar file's full path. The caller gives no guarantees about the sidecar: absence, unreadability, and malformed JSON content are all tolerated input states, not error conditions.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the UTF-8-decoded JSON value parsed from the file whose path is the direct concatenation of file_path and suffix, when that file exists, can be opened, and its content parses as JSON; the parsed value is returned unchanged, without schema validation or transformation. Returns None when the concatenated-path file does not exist, cannot be opened or read, or its content fails to parse as JSON. Never raises for absent, unreadable, or JSON-invalid input states: every I/O-open/read failure and every JSON decode failure on the sidecar maps to a None return instead. Sidecar absence is therefore indistinguishable in the return value from malformed content, and distinguishing them is never required of callers.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Let sidecar_path = f"{file_path}{suffix}" and let result denote the return value of _load_sidecar_json. Exactly one of the following holds: (1) The file at sidecar_path was successfully opened for reading with UTF-8 encoding and its content constituted a valid JSON document; in this case result equals the Python object produced by json.load on that content (i.e., result is one of dict, list, str, int, float, bool, or None as dictated by the JSON grammar), and no exception is raised. (2) An OSError occurred while attempting to open or read sidecar_path (covering absence, permission denial, I/O failure, etc.) or a json.JSONDecodeError occurred while parsing the file content; in this case result is None and no exception propagates to the caller. In all cases: (a) no OSError or json.JSONDecodeError escapes the function; (b) the function performs no writes, so the filesystem state and the objects bound to file_path and suffix are unchanged; (c) the opened file handle, if any, is deterministically closed via the with-statement before the function returns. Note: exceptions outside the caught set (e.g., UnicodeDecodeError for non-UTF-8 byte sequences, MemoryError, TypeError if file_path or suffix lack __format__) are not suppressed and may propagate to the caller.

</details>

- 触发/冲突：A '#' that is the first non-whitespace character of its line retains only the '#' itself; a '//' marker occurring later on the same line is still stripped (input '# hello // world' yields '# hello ' instead of the spec-required verbatim line '# hello // world').
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：code is the full source text of one extracted function, read verbatim from its file. The text may be empty and may contain any mixture of comments, string literals, and code lines.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a text derived from code in which all comments are removed: every block comment spanning from '/*' to its matching '*/' (or, when no '*/' follows, to the end of code), every '//' line comment from its marker to the end of its line, and every '#' comment from its marker to the end of its line are deleted; however, a '#' starts a comment only when a non-whitespace character precedes it on the same line, so a '#' that is the first non-whitespace character of its line, together with the rest of that line, is retained. All text outside comments is preserved character-for-character except for lines dropped as described below. The contents of string literals delimited by single or double quotes, including escape sequences and comment-like sequences, are preserved byte-for-byte: comment markers occurring inside a string literal have no effect, and an escape sequence (a backslash followed by one character) inside a string literal preserves both characters of the sequence and does not terminate the literal. After comment removal, every line that is empty or contains only whitespace is dropped entirely, and the surviving lines are returned in their original relative order with exactly one '\n' between adjacent lines and no trailing newline. Returns the empty string when no line survives. The transformation is pure and raises no exception for any string input.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string derived from the input `code` after removing comments and blank lines. Specifically: (1) All block comments delimited by `/*` and `*/` are removed, except that newline characters within them are preserved. (2) All single-line comments starting with `//` are removed from the `//` to the end of that line. (3) All inline hash comments starting with `#` are removed from `#` to end of line, but ONLY when `#` does not appear at the logical start of a line (i.e., preceded only by whitespace since the last newline); a `#` at line-start is preserved as literal content. (4) All string literals (delimited by matching `"` or `'`) are preserved verbatim, including any comment-like sequences or escape sequences within them. (5) After comment removal, the resulting text is split on newlines, all lines that are empty or contain only whitespace are discarded, and the remaining lines are joined with `\n`. Formally: let R be the character sequence produced by the state-machine loop; then return value = '\n'.join([l for l in ''.join(R).split('\n') if l.strip()]). The input `code` is not mutated (Python str is immutable). No exception is raised for any valid string input, including the empty string (which yields an empty string result). If `code` is empty, the return value is the empty string ''. The local variables `result`, `index`, `in_block_comment`, `in_string`, …

</details>

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：phases_dir is a directory containing a readable phases.json that is the current plan: a JSON object with a "phases" array in which every phase carries an integer "phase" number and a "modules" array, and every module carries a "source_files" list of project-relative source-path strings and, for reporting purposes, a "name" string. Phases may share one or more source files, either across different phases or repeated within the plan; the plan is otherwise well-formed.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：After return, the phases.json on disk in phases_dir contains the same plan as before except that no source file is owned anywhere in the plan more than once: for every source file, its earliest occurrence in plan order (phases in ascending "phase" number, then modules in listed order, then files in listed order) is kept and every later occurrence is removed from the source_files list of the module that held it. The order of the retained files inside each module is preserved, no file is added anywhere, and the file written to disk is a JSON document readable by the same schema. Nothing besides source_files lists is changed: every phase and every module remains present with its number, name, description, and depends_on_phases intact even if its source_files list becomes empty, so phase numbering stays aligned with already-produced per-phase artifacts. The function returns a dict containing a "modified_modules" key whose value is a list holding one entry per module that lost at least one duplicate file; each entry records that module's phase number (the same numbering as in the freshly written phases.json), its name, the files removed from it, and its remaining source files. The list is empty exactly when the plan already had no duplicate source file.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：Upon normal termination: (1) The file at os.path.join(phases_dir, 'phases.json') has been overwritten (via json.dump with indent=2) with a JSON object whose structure mirrors the original plan except that every module's 'source_files' list has been filtered. (2) Deduplication invariant: for every source-file string sf that appeared anywhere in the original plan, sf appears in the 'source_files' list of exactly one module in the written JSON specifically the module belonging to the phase with the smallest 'phase' number (and, within that phase, the earliest module iteration order) that originally contained sf. No source file appears in more than one module's 'source_files' in the output. (3) Structural preservation: the set of phases (identified by their 'phase' integer) and the set of modules within each phase are identical to those in the input; no phase or module object is added or removed. Phase numbers and all keys other than 'source_files' are unchanged. A module whose 'source_files' becomes empty is still present. (4) Return value: a dict with a single key 'modified_modules' mapping to a list of dicts. Each entry corresponds to one module whose 'source_files' list shrank (i.e., at least one file was removed) and contains: 'phase' (int, the phase number), 'module' (str, the module's 'name' or '' if absent), 'removed_files' (list of str, the source-file paths stripped from…

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-108 — `src--plugin-py--_resolve_command`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/plugin-py/_resolve_command.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/plugin-py/_resolve_command.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--plugin-py--_resolve_command.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--plugin-py--_resolve_command.py)
- 触发/冲突：The spec requires tokens starting with '/', '$', or '-' to be preserved unchanged, but the prefix guard checks only '/' and '$', so a token like '-flag' that names an existing file under plugin_root is incorrectly rewritten to that file's absolute path instead of being left as-is.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：cmd is a raw shell command string supplied by a plugin stage configuration. plugin_root is the root directory of the plugin that owns cmd; relative file references in cmd are intended to resolve against plugin_root.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a command string directly executable by a shell whose whitespace-separated token sequence is the token sequence of cmd transformed token by token, preserving order and token count: a token whose first character is '/', '$', or '-' appears in the result unchanged regardless of whether it names a file; otherwise, a token that names an existing regular file when joined to plugin_root appears in the result as that file's absolute path string; every remaining token appears unchanged. Consequently every relative token that named an existing file under plugin_root now names it absolutely, no token that failed to name such a file is altered, and the result makes no fewer file-reference guarantees than cmd.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a string `result` such that: 1. Let `tokens = cmd.split()` (whitespace-delimited splitting, collapsing multiple whitespace characters and stripping leading/trailing whitespace). Let `n = len(tokens)`. 2. `result` is the concatenation of `n` processed tokens joined by single space characters: `result == ' '.join(resolved)` where `resolved` is a list of length `n`. 3. For each index `i` in `range(n)`, let `t = tokens[i]`: - If `t.startswith('/')` or `t.startswith('$')`: `resolved[i] == t` (token is preserved unchanged). - Else, let `candidate = plugin_root / t` (pathlib join): - If `candidate.is_file()` evaluates to `True`: `resolved[i] == str(candidate)` (token is replaced with the absolute/resolved path string). - Else: `resolved[i] == t` (token is preserved unchanged). 4. The relative ordering of tokens is preserved. 5. If `cmd` is empty or contains only whitespace, `tokens` is empty and the function returns the empty string `''`. 6. Note: Despite the docstring mentioning tokens starting with `'-'` are left unchanged, the implementation does NOT special-case `'-'`; such tokens proceed through the `plugin_root / token` file-existence check. 7. No explicit exceptions are raised by the function logic itself. However, `candidate.is_file()` may propagate `OSError` (e.g., permission denied) and `plugin_root / token` may raise `TypeError` if `token` is not a val…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-111 — `src--reasoner-py--_compute_brace_depth_per_line`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/reasoner-py/_compute_brace_depth_per_line.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/reasoner-py/_compute_brace_depth_per_line.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--reasoner-py--_compute_brace_depth_per_line.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--reasoner-py--_compute_brace_depth_per_line.py)
- 触发/冲突：A block comment opened on one line and closed on a later line (e.g. ["/* {", "} */"]) is not tracked across lines, so a closing brace on the comment-continuation portion of a later line is counted as ordinary code and shifts all subsequent depths by one, violating the spec-required [0, 0] depths for that input.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：lines is the ordered list of source lines of a single function, with any line-number prefixes and leading whitespace removed. Every element is a string, possibly empty, and the list itself may be empty.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a list of integers with exactly one element per input line, in the same order as the input. The element at index i equals the cumulative brace nesting depth at the end of line i: the number of block-opening brace characters encountered in lines 0 through i minus the number of block-closing brace characters encountered in the same text. Depth is accumulated cumulatively in line order, so values remain consistent for constructs that span multiple lines. A brace character contributes to the depth only when it appears in ordinary code text: braces inside string literals, inside character literals, inside the remainder of a line after the start of a line comment, and inside any portion of a block comment lying within the current line contribute nothing to the depth. Escape sequences within string and character literals do not terminate the literal early, and an unterminated literal or block comment contributes no further depth for the rest of that line. An empty input list yields an empty result list. The function mutates its argument not at all, performs no I/O, raises no exceptions for the stated input, and produces no effects beyond the returned list.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a list `result` such that: (1) len(result) == len(lines); (2) the input list `lines` is not mutated; (3) no exception is raised assuming every element of `lines` is a str; (4) for each index i in range(len(lines)), result[i] equals the cumulative brace depth after sequentially processing lines[0] through lines[i], where the depth starts at 0 and is updated by scanning each line left-to-right character-by-character with the following rules: (a) characters inside a double-quoted string literal (delimited by '"', with backslash-escape sequences skipping the next character) are ignored; (b) characters inside a single-quoted char literal (delimited by "'", with backslash-escape sequences skipping the next character) are ignored; (c) upon encountering '//' outside strings/chars, the remainder of that line is ignored; (d) upon encountering '/*' outside strings/chars, characters are skipped until '*/' is found or the line ends (block comments do NOT span multiple lines in this simplified implementation); (e) each '{' encountered outside all the above contexts increments depth by 1; (f) each '}' encountered outside all the above contexts decrements depth by 1; (5) if lines is empty, result is the empty list []; (6) depth may become negative if closing braces exceed opening braces; (7) unterminated string/char literals or unterminated block comments within a line si…

</details>
