- 触发/冲突：For a language without a dedicated _TERMINATING_PATTERNS entry (e.g. 'zig'), the generic fallback pattern requires whitespace after raise/throw ('raise\s', 'throw\s'), so a bare 'raise' or 'throw' at the end of a block with no trailing whitespace fails to match and _has_terminating_statement returns False where the spec requires True.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：block is a possibly multi-line string of function body text (line-numbered statements carrying 'Line N: ' prefixes); language is the display name of the source language, as a string. Neither argument is required to be non-empty.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns True iff block contains, on at least one of its lines, a statement that terminates the enclosing function's control flow on some execution path under the given language's semantics — returning control to the caller, raising/throwing an exception or error signal, or invoking a primitive that aborts the process. A statement qualifies regardless of its position within block. Languages that define a recognized termination vocabulary are matched by that vocabulary; languages without a dedicated vocabulary are matched by a generic vocabulary covering the same semantic classes (return to caller, exception/error raising or throwing, process abort). Returns False only when no such statement occurs anywhere in block, in which case the caller defers the spec-discharge obligation to a later block. The result depends only on block, language, and their termination vocabularies; neither argument is modified.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a boolean value. Let lang_key = language.lower(). If _TERMINATING_PATTERNS.get(lang_key) yields a truthy value p, then the return value equals (re.search(p, block) is not None). Otherwise (the key is absent or maps to a falsy value), the return value equals (re.search(r'\b(return\b|exit\s*\(|raise\s|throw\s|abort\s*\()', block) is not None). In all cases the result is True iff the chosen regex pattern matches at least one position in block, and False otherwise. No mutation of block, language, or _TERMINATING_PATTERNS occurs. Exceptions: AttributeError is raised if language is not a string (violating the pre-condition); TypeError is raised if block is not a string or None; re.error may be raised if the pattern retrieved from _TERMINATING_PATTERNS is not a valid regular expression. Under the stated pre-condition (both arguments are strings, possibly empty), the function terminates normally and returns a bool.

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-113 — `src--reasoner-py--_parse_spec_conditions`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/reasoner-py/_parse_spec_conditions.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/reasoner-py/_parse_spec_conditions.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--reasoner-py--_parse_spec_conditions.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--reasoner-py--_parse_spec_conditions.py)
- 触发/冲突：A spec string containing the 'Pre-condition:' label with an empty body and no trailing newline makes the pre-condition regex (which requires a newline after the label) fail to match, so pre is returned as None instead of the spec-required empty string.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：spec is reasoner-facing specification text, expected to contain up to two sections introduced by the literal labels 'Pre-condition:' and 'Post-condition:'; either label, or both, may be absent from spec.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a pair (pre, post) whose components are strings or None, each determined independently of the other. pre is None iff spec contains no 'Pre-condition:' label; otherwise it is the text of that section, from immediately after the section label up to but excluding the 'Post-condition:' label, or up to the end of spec when the 'Post-condition:' label is absent, with leading and trailing whitespace stripped. post is None iff spec contains no 'Post-condition:' label; otherwise it is the text from immediately after that label to the end of spec, stripped likewise. Beyond the boundary whitespace stripping, the extracted text preserves each section's original wording verbatim — no rewriting, reordering, or truncation. A present section label whose section body is empty yields the empty string for that component. spec is not modified.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a 2-tuple (pre, post). (1) pre is None if and only if the regex r'Pre-condition:\s*\n(.*?)(?=\nPost-condition:|\Z)' does not match spec; otherwise pre equals the stripped content of capture group 1, i.e., the text between the 'Pre-condition:' label (plus optional trailing whitespace and a newline) and either the next '\nPost-condition:' boundary or the end of spec. (2) post is None if and only if the regex r'Post-condition:\s*\n(.*)' does not match spec; otherwise post equals the stripped content of capture group 1, i.e., all text from after 'Post-condition:' (plus optional trailing whitespace and a newline) to the end of spec. (3) The input spec is not mutated. (4) If spec is not a str (or bytes-like object acceptable to re.search), a TypeError is raised and no tuple is returned. (5) Under the stated pre-condition (spec is a string that may contain zero, one, or both section labels), the function always terminates normally and returns the tuple without raising exceptions. Formally: spec str: let m_pre = re.search(r'Pre-condition:\s*\n(.*?)(?=\nPost-condition:|\Z)', spec, DOTALL), m_post = re.search(r'Post-condition:\s*\n(.*)', spec, DOTALL) in result = (m_pre.group(1).strip() if m_pre else None, m_post.group(1).strip() if m_post else None).

</details>

Every string appearing anywhere in the result — values and dict keys alike — contains only ASCII characters: each returned string equals the original string with every non-ASCII code point removed, so it is character-for-character identical to the original exactly when the original was already ASCII-only. Non-string leaves (numbers, booleans, None) are returned unchanged. obj and any containers nested inside it are not mutated. The result shares no mutable container with obj (independent deep copy).
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a new value `result` such that: (1) `result` is JSON-serializable. (2) The original `obj` is not mutated; all dicts and lists in `result` are freshly constructed objects. (3) The structural shape is preserved: for every dict in `obj`, there is a corresponding dict in `result` with the same set of keys (keys are NOT sanitized); for every list in `obj`, there is a corresponding list in `result` of the same length; nesting depth and arrangement are identical. (4) Every string leaf value in `result` contains only ASCII characters (i.e., for every string s in result, all(c <= '\x7f' for c in s) holds), obtained by stripping non-ASCII code points from the corresponding string in `obj`. (5) Non-string leaf values (int, float, bool, None) are returned unchanged (identity or equality preserved). (6) Formally: path p into the nested structure, if obj[p] is a str then result[p] == obj[p].encode('ascii','ignore').decode('ascii'); if obj[p] is a non-str scalar then result[p] == obj[p]; if obj[p] is a dict then result[p].keys() == obj[p].keys(); if obj[p] is a list then len(result[p]) == len(obj[p]). (7) No exception is raised under the stated pre-condition. (8) The caller may persist `result` as JSON and rely on `obj` remaining unmodified.

For each index i in range(n), letting f = funcs_info[i]: (1) sig_line = source_lines[f['start'] - 1].strip(); (2) raw_doc = f.get('docstring', '') or '' (yielding '' if the key is absent, None, or any other falsy value); (3) doc = (raw_doc.split('\n')[0])[:120] if raw_doc is truthy, else ''; (4) the i-th entry equals the string f"- {f['name']} | {sig_line} | {doc}". No exceptions are raised under the stated pre-conditions (every 'start' is a valid 1-based index into source_lines, every 'name' is a non-empty string). The function has no side effects beyond returning the computed string.

</details>

- 触发/冲突：Acronym-leading names such as 'ABTest': the CamelCase splitter inserts an underscore before every uppercase letter, so the two-character case-change segment 'ab' is filtered out and never appears among the name parts.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：name is a function or class name string following the language's identifier conventions (letters, digits, and separator characters, in any letter case). name may be empty only if the caller tolerates a result containing no identifier parts.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns a set of lowercase identifier tokens derived from the structural decomposition of name, suitable for overlap matching (set intersection) against lowercased intent-derived identifier sets. Every element of the result is a lowercase, non-empty string. The set contains: (1) the entire name lowercased, with its separators preserved; (2) every separator-delimited identifier segment of name that is at least two characters long, lowercased; (3) for every adjacent pair in the sequence of separator-delimited segments of name that are at least two characters long, the compound formed by joining that pair with the separator character, lowercased; (4) every segment of at least two characters obtained by splitting name at letter case-change boundaries, lowercased. Separator characters and segments shorter than two characters contribute no tokens, so no token consisting solely of separators or single characters ever appears. Tokens overlap by design: the same identifier characters may appear in the whole-name token, in compound tokens, and in segment tokens simultaneously. Because every returned element is lowercase, the letter case of name influences the result only insofar as it marks component boundaries in camel-cased spellings. The argument is not mutated, and no exception is raised on well-formed input.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function terminates normally and returns a set of strings `parts`. The following invariants hold: (1) `name.lower()` parts (the full lowercased input is always present, even if name is empty, in which case the empty string is included). (2) For every token t obtained by splitting `name.lower()` on '_' with len(t) > 1, t parts. (3) For every pair of consecutive such tokens (toks[i], toks[i+1]), the compound string f"{toks[i]}_{toks[i+1]}" parts. (4) For every segment p obtained by inserting '_' before each uppercase letter in name (via regex `([A-Z])` `_\1`), lowercasing, stripping leading/trailing underscores, and splitting on '_', if len(p) > 1 then p parts. (5) Every element s parts satisfies s == s.lower() (all elements are lowercase). (6) parts is non-empty (it contains at least `name.lower()`). (7) No exception is raised given the pre-condition that name is a valid identifier string (letters, digits, separators). Formally: s parts: s.islower() s == ''; name.lower() parts; t [x for x in name.lower().split('_') if len(x)>1]: t parts; i range(len(toks)-1): toks[i]+'_'+toks[i+1] parts; p [x for x in re.sub(r'([A-Z])',r'_\1',name).lower().strip('_').split('_') if len(x)>1]: p parts; len(parts) 1.

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-125 — `src--verification-py--_spec_task_exit_code`

- 结论：**SPEC 错误**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/verification-py/_spec_task_exit_code.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/verification-py/_spec_task_exit_code.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--verification-py--_spec_task_exit_code.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--verification-py--_spec_task_exit_code.py)
- 触发/冲突：A future-style handle whose result() raises SystemExit(1) causes _spec_task_exit_code to propagate the BaseException instead of returning 1, because the handler catches only Exception.
- 成因复核：差异能够复现，但被违反的要求主要来自生成 SPEC 的无依据强化，不能直接据此修改实现。

<details><summary>SPEC / POST 摘录</summary>

- Pre-condition：handle is one element of spec_procs representing a spec-generation task; queried after the handle is reported done.
- Qwen SPEC post-condition：Returns the termination exit code of the represented spec-generation task with unified semantics regardless of which handle kind is supplied: zero denotes successful completion and any non-zero value denotes failure. For a process-style handle, the returned value is the recorded termination status, negative when the process was terminated by a signal. For a future-style handle, the returned value is the task's reported exit code when its outcome carries one; when the task finished without reporting an exit code, zero is returned, and when the task's outcome cannot be retrieved at all, a non-zero code (1) is returned instead of raising. Returns None when the handle provides no termination status. The function never raises an exception for a handle of either kind.
- Qwen 推导 actual POST：The function returns a value `r` representing the normalized exit status of the spec-generation task `handle`. Exactly one of the following holds: 1. If `handle` has a `returncode` attribute (Popen-style): `r == handle.returncode`. Given the pre-condition that the task is done, this is an `int` (0 for success, non-zero for failure), though the type is not statically enforced. 2. Else if `handle` has a callable `done` attribute and `handle.done()` is truthy (Future-style): a. If `handle.result()` completes without exception and returns an `int`: `r` equals that `int`. b. If `handle.result()` completes without exception and returns a non-`int` value: `r == 0`. c. If `handle.result()` raises any `Exception`: `r == 1`. 3. Otherwise (handle matches neither interface): `r is None`. Formally: handle spec_procs, done(handle): let r = _spec_task_exit_code(handle) in (hasattr(handle,'returncode') r = handle.returncode) (hasattr(handle,'returncode') hasattr(handle,'done') handle.done() (v. handle.result()=v isinstance(v,int) r=v) (v. handle.result()=v isinstance(v,int) r=0) (handle.result() raises Exception r=1)) (hasattr(handle,'returncode') (hasattr(handle,'done') handle.done()) r is None) No exceptions propagate to the caller; all exceptions from `handle.result()` are caught. The function has no side effects on `handle` or global state beyond invoking `handle.done()` and `handle.resul…

</details>

#### QWEN-MISMATCH-127 — `src--verification-py--streaming_reasoner`

- 结论：**实现缺陷候选**；内置 validator：`confirmed`。
- 证据：[`[SPEC]` 原文](../fm_agent/extracted_functions/src/verification-py/streaming_reasoner.py) · [推导 POST / result](../fm_agent/logic_verification_results/src/verification-py/streaming_reasoner.json) · [具体 bug 报告](../fm_agent/bug_validation/src--verification-py--streaming_reasoner.md) · [probe](../fm_agent/bug_validation/probe_src--verification-py--streaming_reasoner.py)
- 触发/冲突：A user interruption (KeyboardInterrupt) arriving while a reasoning future is in flight causes the interrupt handler to discard the future's MISMATCH verdict without submitting it to bug validation, even though proj_dir is provided.
- 成因复核：反例位于可信输入域，并涉及数据完整性、格式协议、真实调用链、跨平台或资源/安全边界，值得补独立回归测试。

Only MISMATCH verdicts trigger bug validation; when proj_dir is provided, every MISMATCH is submitted to bug validation before return. ## Validator 完整性说明

主运行结束时有 8 个 MISMATCH 因 OpenCode/Qwen 操作超时而缺少 validator 结果；这些调用均在结果落盘前以 exit code 1 退出。补试后 8 项全部完成，其中 6 confirmed、2 not_confirmed。最终 127 条 MISMATCH 无缺失 validator、无 validator error。详见 [conclu.md](conclu.md)。

## 复核边界

本清单不把生成 SPEC 自动视作 ground truth，也没有在缺乏产品契约时擅自修改源码。实现缺陷候选应逐项转换为独立回归测试；契约待确认应先由维护者决定行为；SPEC 错误和推理误判应进入验证器评测集。
